As a result, keyword-based search often fails to capture design intent, and general-purpose vision-language retrieval models trained on natural images are ill-suited to the text-heavy, multi-component nature of infographics. To address these challenges, we develop an intent-aware infographic retrieval framework that better aligns user queries with infographic designs. We first conduct a formative study of how people describe infographics and derive an intent taxonomy spanning content and visual design facets. This taxonomy is then leveraged to enrich and refine free-form user queries, guiding the retrieval process with intent-specific cues. Building on the retrieved exemplars, users can adapt the designs to their own data with high-level edit intents, supported by an interactive agent that performs low-level adaptation. Both quantitative evaluations and user studies are conducted to demonstrate that our method improves retrieval quality over baseline methods while better supporting intent satisfaction and efficient infographic authoring.

</details>


### [196] [Context-Aware Disentanglement for Cross-Domain Sequential Recommendation: A Causal View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07992)
*Xingzi Wang,Qingtian Bian,Hui Fang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CoDiS，一个基于因果视角的跨域序列推荐上下文感知解耦框架，通过变分上下文调整减少混杂偏差、专家隔离选择策略解决梯度冲突、以及变分对抗解耦模块分离领域共享与领域特定偏好，在三个真实数据集上显著超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域序列推荐方法存在三个主要局限：(1)忽视用户交互序列中上下文变化导致的虚假相关性；(2)领域共享与领域特定偏好的学习受梯度冲突影响产生"跷跷板效应"；(3)依赖用户大量重叠这一不现实假设。这些问题限制了推荐系统在数据稀疏和冷启动场景下的性能。

Method: CoDiS框架包含三个核心组件：①变分上下文调整方法（Variational Context Adjustment）减少上下文的混杂效应；②专家隔离与选择策略（Expert Isolation and Selection）解决梯度冲突问题；③变分对抗解耦模块（Variational Adversarial Disentangling）彻底分离领域共享与领域特定表征。整个框架基于因果推断视角构建。

Result: 在三个真实世界数据集上的广泛实验表明，CoDiS在统计显著性意义上一致性地优于最先进的CDSR基线方法。

Conclusion: CoDiS通过因果驱动的解耦机制有效解决了跨域推荐中的虚假相关、梯度冲突和用户重叠假设问题，实验验证了其有效性和优越性。

Abstract: Cross-Domain Sequential Recommendation (CDSR) aims to en-hance recommendation quality by transferring knowledge across domains, offering effective solutions to data sparsity and cold-start issues. However, existing methods face three major limitations: (1) they overlook varying contexts in user interaction sequences, resulting in spurious correlations that obscure the true causal relationships driving user preferences; (2) the learning of domain- shared and domain-specific preferences is hindered by gradient conflicts between domains, leading to a seesaw effect where performance in one domain improves at the expense of the other; (3) most methods rely on the unrealistic assumption of substantial user overlap across domains. To address these issues, we propose CoDiS, a context-aware disentanglement framework grounded in a causal view to accurately disentangle domain-shared and domain-specific preferences. Specifically, Our approach includes a variational context adjustment method to reduce confounding effects of contexts, expert isolation and selection strategies to resolve gradient conflict, and a variational adversarial disentangling module for the thorough disentanglement of domain-shared and domain-specific representations. Extensive experiments on three real-world datasets demonstrate that CoDiS consistently outperforms state-of-the-art CDSR baselines with statistical significance. Code is available at:https://anonymous.4open.science/r/CoDiS-6FA0.

</details>


### [197] [Search Changes Consumers' Minds: How Recognizing Gaps Drives Sustainable Choices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08079)
*Frans van der Sluis,Leif Azzopardi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究探讨信息搜索如何弥合消费者在负责任购物中的意向-行为差距。通过308名参与者的任务研究，发现主动搜索道德信息能提升消费者对伦理维度的重视程度，但真正驱动购买决策改变的是对伦理问题的认知与理解，而非单纯的信息搜索行为。意识到自身知识缺口是促进行为改变的关键催化剂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费者虽有意愿进行负责任购物，但常难以将意向转化为实际行为。研究旨在验证信息搜索是否能帮助消费者做出更符合其价值观的购买决策。

Method: 任务导向研究，308名参与者针对其正在选购的商品，搜索八个伦理维度的信息之一。采用实验设计追踪搜索行为、伦理认知与购买决策的变化。

Result: 主动搜索伦理信息整体提升了参与者对伦理维度的重视程度。然而，驱动购买行为向更负责任方向转变的关键因素是伦理认知与理解，而非伦理意向或搜索活动本身。识别并填补知识缺口的参与者表现出显著的行为改变，包括增加搜索行为及更强的未来购物习惯改变意愿。

Conclusion: 负责任消费可视为部分信息问题。消费者意识到自身知识局限性可能是促成有意义行为改变的关键催化剂，提示消费者教育的重点应放在激发元认知觉醒而非单纯信息提供。

Abstract: Despite a growing desire among consumers to shop responsibly, translating this intention into behaviour remains challenging. Previous work has identified that information seeking (or lack thereof) is a contributing factor to this intention-behaviour gap.In this paper, we hypothesize that searching can bridge this gap - helping consumers to make purchasing decisions that are better aligned with their values. We conducted a task-based study with 308 participants, asking them to search for information on one of eight ethical aspects regarding a product they were actively shopping for. Our findings show that actively searching for such information led to an overall increase in the importance participants' assigned to ethical aspects.However, it was the recognition and understanding of ethical considerations, rather than ethical intentions or search activity, that drove shifts towards more responsible purchasing decisions. Participants who acknowledged and filled knowledge gaps in their decision making showed significant behaviour change, including increased searching and a stronger desire to alter their future shopping habits. We conclude that responsible consumption can be considered a partial information problem, where awareness of one's own knowledge limitations may be the catalyst needed for meaningful consumer behaviour change.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [Valve: Production Online-Offline Inference Colocation with Jointly-Bounded Preemption Latency and Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07874)
*Fangyue Liu,Hua Liu,Xinyuan Lyu,Shuo Ai,Hao Liang,Lingpeng Chen,Ziqian Hu,Chong Zha,Xin Jin,Hanmei Luo,Peng Chen*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: Valve是一个生产级LLM推理共置系统，通过GPU运行时实现亚毫秒级计算抢占和受限速率的内存回收，在8054个GPU上部署后实现34.6%的集群利用率提升，节省2170个GPU，同时将TTFT增加控制在5%以内，TPOT增加控制在2%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理流量的突发性导致过度配置和资源利用不足，在线-离线任务共置虽能利用空闲容量，但面临大在线干扰（慢速或频繁抢占）和需大量框架/驱动修改两大挑战，限制了其大规模生产部署。

Method: Valve提出三大核心机制：通道控制的计算隔离（channel-controlled compute isolation）、无页错误的内存回收（page-fault-free memory reclamation）和动态内存预留（dynamic memory reservation）。系统保证每个在线请求最多一次亚毫秒级计算抢占，并限制子层内存回收速率。部署仅需1行驱动修改和20行框架补丁。

Result: 在8054个GPU上生产部署验证，集群利用率提升34.6%，相当于节省2170个GPU。运行时干扰极小，TTFT增加<5%，TPOT增加<2%。

Conclusion: Valve成功证明了生产级友好共置系统的可行性，通过轻量级修改和精细化资源管理，在保持在线服务质量的同时显著提升资源利用效率。

Abstract: LLM inference powers latency-critical production services nowadays. The bursty nature of inference traffic results in over-provisioning, which in turn leads to resource underutilization. While online-offline colocation promises to utilize idle capacity, broad production deployment must overcome two major challenges: (i) large online interference due to slow or frequent preemptions, and (ii) extensive frameworks and drivers modifications, to colocate different models and support preemptions. We present Valve, a production-friendly colocation system that jointly bounds preemption latency and preemption rate. Specifically, Valve enables sub-millisecond compute preemption at most once per online request, and rate-limited sub-layer memory reclamation. These guaranties are provided by a GPU runtime that combines channel-controlled compute isolation, page-fault-free memory reclamation, and dynamic memory reservation. Critically, Valve is practical to deploy, requiring one line of driver modification and 20 lines of framework patch. Deployed on 8,054 GPUs in production, Valve improves cluster utilization by 34.6%, which translates to a 2,170 GPU save. This efficiency gains is achieved with minimal online interference, incurring <5% TTFT increase and <2% TPOT increase across workloads.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [199] [SynQL: A Controllable and Scalable Rule-Based Framework for SQL Workload Synthesis for Performance Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08021)
*Kahan Mehta,Amit Mankodi*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出SynQL框架，通过遍历外键图结构而非概率文本生成来合成SQL工作负载，解决隐私限制下训练数据不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据库研究和学习查询优化器需要真实SQL工作负载，但隐私法规限制获取，且公开的匿名化痕迹通常剥离可执行查询文本。传统基准模板过少无法统计泛化，LLM方法存在模式幻觉和拓扑坍缩问题。

Method: SynQL采用确定性框架，通过遍历数据库外键图填充AST，而非概率文本生成。使用配置向量Θ显式控制连接拓扑(星型、链型、叉型)、分析强度和谓词选择性，保证模式和句法有效性。

Result: 在TPC-H和IMDb上，SynQL生成接近最大多样性的工作负载(拓扑熵H=1.53 bits)，基于合成语料训练的树模型在保留测试集上R²≥0.79，推理延迟亚毫秒级。

Conclusion: SynQL可有效解决生产日志不可用时的训练数据生成问题，是学习查询优化器的有效基础。

Abstract: Database research and the development of learned query optimisers rely heavily on realistic SQL workloads. Acquiring real-world queries is increasingly difficult, however, due to strict privacy regulations, and publicly released anonymised traces typically strip out executable query text to preserve confidentiality. Existing synthesis tools fail to bridge this training data gap: traditional benchmarks offer too few fixed templates for statistical generalisation, while Large Language Model (LLM) approaches suffer from schema hallucination fabricating non-existent columns and topological collapse systematically defaulting to simplistic join patterns that fail to stress-test query optimisers. We propose SynQL, a deterministic workload synthesis framework that generates structurally diverse, execution-ready SQL workloads. As a foundational step toward bridging the training-data gap, SynQL targets the core SQL fragment -- multi-table joins with projections, aggregations, and range predicates -- which dominates analytical workloads. SynQL abandons probabilistic text generation in favour of traversing the live database's foreign-key graph to populate an Abstract Syntax Tree (AST), guaranteeing schema and syntactic validity by construction. A configuration vector $Θ$ provides explicit, parametric control over join topology (Star, Chain, Fork), analytical intensity, and predicate selectivity. Experiments on TPC-H and IMDb show that SynQL produces near-maximally diverse workloads (Topological Entropy $H = 1.53$ bits) and that tree-based cost models trained on the synthetic corpus achieve $R^2 \ge 0.79$ on held-out synthetic test sets with sub-millisecond inference latency, establishing SynQL as an effective foundation for generating training data when production logs are inaccessible.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [200] [Identifying bubble-like subgraphs in linear-time via a unified SPQR-tree framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08071)
*Francisco Sena,Aleksandr Politov,Corentin Moumard,Massimo Cairo,Romeo Rizzi,Manuel Cáceres,Sebastian Schmidt,Juha Harviainen,Alexandru I. Tomescu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文解决了计算生物学中在有向图或双向图中识别所有snarls和ultrabubbles的开放问题，首次提出线性时间算法。核心方法利用SPQR树分解编码双连通图的所有2-分离器，结合动态规划遍历和反馈弧集计算。通过证明tipless双向图中所有反馈弧可在线性时间内计算这一基础性结果，实现了整体线性时间复杂度。该框架还可统一导出superbubbles的线性时间算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在计算生物学中，在给定有向图或双向图中识别特定子图（superbubbles、snarls和ultrabubbles）是一个基础算法问题。这些子图对应于遗传变异区域，对分析基因组集合非常有价值。该问题自2018年以来一直是开放问题。

Method: 1. 利用SPQR树分解来编码双连通图的所有2-分离器；2. 对SPQR树进行多次动态规划风格的遍历；3. 维护关键性质（如无环性）以判断给定的2-分离器是否定义了应报告的子图；4. 核心创新：证明在tipless双向图中，所有反馈弧可在线性时间内计算（这是实现线性时间复杂度的关键）。

Result: 首次提出识别所有snarls和所有ultrabubbles的线性时间算法，解决了自2018年以来的开放问题。同时提供了一个统一的框架，还能导出superbubbles的完全不同的线性时间算法。

Conclusion: 本文的框架统一解决了三类bubbles的识别问题。虽然部分技术细节较为复杂，但核心思想概念简单，并可能推广到其他类似bubbles的子图。研究表明SPQR树分解可作为设计高效算法的通用工具，超越其传统的图绘制角色，为计算生物学理论基础做出贡献。

Abstract: A fundamental algorithmic problem in computational biology is to find all subgraphs of a given type (superbubbles, snarls, and ultrabubbles) in a directed or bidirected input graph. These correspond to regions of genetic variation and are useful in analyzing collections of genomes. We present the first linear-time algorithms for identifying all snarls and all ultrabubbles, resolving problems open since 2018. The algorithm for snarls relies on a new linear-size representation of all snarls with respect to the number of vertices in the graph. We employ the well-known SPQR-tree decomposition, which encodes all 2-separators of a biconnected graph. After several dynamic-programming-style traversals of this tree, we maintain key properties (such as acyclicity) that allow us to decide whether a given 2-separator defines a subgraph to be reported. A crucial ingredient for linear-time complexity is that acyclicity of linearly many subgraphs can be tested simultaneously via the problem of computing all arcs in a directed graph whose removal renders it acyclic (so-called feedback arcs). As such, we prove a fundamental result for bidirected graphs, that may be of independent interest: all feedback arcs can be computed in linear time for tipless bidirected graphs, while in general this is at least as hard as matrix multiplication, assuming the k-Clique Conjecture. Our results form a unified framework that also yields a completely different linear-time algorithm for finding all superbubbles. Although some of the results are technically involved, the underlying ideas are conceptually simple, and may extend to other bubble-like subgraphs. More broadly, our work contributes to the theoretical foundations of computational biology and advances a growing line of research that uses SPQR-tree decompositions as a general tool for designing efficient algorithms, beyond their traditional role in graph drawing.

</details>


### [201] [Counting HyperGraphlets via Color Coding: a Quadratic Barrier and How to Break It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08278)
*Marco Bressan,Stefano Clemente,Giacomo Fumagalli*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究超图上k-超图元（k-hypergraphlet）的计数问题。针对颜色编码技术在超图上的应用，证明了其存在二次屏障（quadratic barrier），即在正交向量猜想下无法达到亚二次时间。随后引入(α,β)-nice性质，使真实世界超图可被高效处理，提出2^O(k)·(2^β|V|+α^k|E|+α^2β|H|)的算法，并在实验中比朴素二次算法快一个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: k-超图元计数是一个重要但被忽视的基本问题。颜色编码是图中k-图元近似计数的经典技术，但其在超图上的效率尚不清楚，需要系统研究。

Method: （1）复杂性下界证明：利用正交向量猜想（Orthogonal Vector Conjecture）证明颜色编码在超图上面临二次屏障；（2）引入(α,β)-niceness性质：可将超图分为两个子超图，分别满足秩≤α和度≤β；（3）分治策略：对不同子超图应用不同技术并组合输出。

Result: （1）证明了在正交向量猜想下，颜色编码在超图上无法达到亚二次时间；（2）对于(α,β)-nice超图，提出时间复杂度为2^O(k)·(2^β|V|+α^k|E|+α^2β|H|)的算法；（3）可期望均匀采样k-超图元，时间为k^O(k)·(β^2+ln|V|)每样本；（4）真实超图实验显示算法明显优于朴素二次算法（超出一个数量级）。

Conclusion: 颜色编码在一般超图上存在固有的计算障碍，但真实世界超图常满足(α,β)-niceness性质，可通过针对性算法克服此障碍，实现高效的超图元计数与采样。

Abstract: We study the problem of counting $k$-\emph{hyper}graphlets, an interesting but surprisingly ignored primitive, with the aim of understanding if efficient algorithms exist. To this end we consider \emph{color coding}, a well-known technique for approximately counting $k$-graphlets in graphs. Our first result is that, on hypergraphs, color coding encounters a \emph{quadratic barrier}: under the Orthogonal Vector Conjecture, no implementation of it can run in time sub-quadratic in the size of the input. We then introduce a simple property, $(α,β)$-niceness, that hypergraphs from real-world datasets appear to satisfy for small values of $α$ and $β$. Intuitively, an $(α,β)$-nice hypergraph can be split into two sub-hypergraphs having respectively rank at most $α$ and degree at most $β$. By applying different techniques to each sub-hypergraph and carefully combining the outputs, we show how to run color coding in time $2^{O(k)} \cdot \big(2^β|V| + α^k |E| + α^2 β\size{H}\big)$, where $H=(V,E)$ is the input hypergraph. Afterwards, we can sample colorful $k$-hypergraphlets uniformly in expected $k^{O(k)} \cdot (β^2+\ln |V|)$ time per sample. Experiments on real-world hypergraphs show that our algorithm neatly outperforms the naive quadratic algorithm, sometimes by more than an order of magnitude.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [ORACLE-SWE: Quantifying the Contribution of Oracle Information Signals on SWE Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07789)
*Kenan Li,Qirui Jin,Liao Zhu,Xiaosong Huang,Yijia Wu,Yikai Zhang,Xin Zhang,Zijian Jin,Yufan Huang,Elsie Nallipogu,Chaoyun Zhang,Yu Kang,Saravan Rajmohan,Qingwei Lin,Wenke Lee,Dongmei Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Oracle-SWE通过隔离提取神谕级别的信息信号，系统性地量化了各上下文信号对语言模型智能体在软件工程任务中的独立贡献，为研究资源分配提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已识别多种有助于SWE任务的信息信号，但各信号的独立贡献度未知，尤其在理想条件下各信号的最佳贡献情况缺乏量化分析。

Method: 提出Oracle-SWE统一框架，从SWE基准中隔离提取神谕级别的信号（复现测试、回归测试、编辑位置、执行上下文、API使用），通过控制变量量化各信号影响，并验证强LLM提取信号的效果。

Result: 明确各信号对智能体性能的独立贡献，建立可量化的影响评估体系。

Conclusion: 为自主编程系统研究提供数据驱动的优先级指导，帮助识别最值得投入的信号和优化方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in language model (LM) agents have significantly improved automated software engineering (SWE). Prior work has proposed various agentic workflows and training strategies as well as analyzed failure modes of agentic systems on SWE tasks, focusing on several contextual information signals: Reproduction Test, Regression Test, Edit Location, Execution Context, and API Usage. However, the individual contribution of each signal to overall success remains underexplored, particularly their ideal contribution when intermediate information is perfectly obtained. To address this gap, we introduce Oracle-SWE, a unified method to isolate and extract oracle information signals from SWE benchmarks and quantify the impact of each signal on agent performance. To further validate the pattern, we evaluate the performance gain of signals extracted by strong LMs when provided to a base agent, approximating real-world task-resolution settings. These evaluations aim to guide research prioritization for autonomous coding systems.

</details>


### [203] [Open-Ended Video Game Glitch Detection with Agentic Reasoning and Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07818)
*Muyang Zheng,Tong Zhou,Geyang Wu,Zihao Lin,Haibo Wang,Lifu Huang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出VideoGlitchBench基准和GliDe框架，用于开放域视频游戏故障检测任务。该任务需要识别游戏视频中的故障、自然语言描述并定位时间跨度，比传统图像级识别更具挑战性，需要理解游戏特有的动态机制（物理、渲染、动画等）。GliDe通过游戏感知上下文记忆、辩论式反射器和事件级定位模块实现更好的故障检测与定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏故障理解任务主要局限于图像级识别或封闭式问答，缺乏对连续游戏视频进行游戏特定动态推理（机制、物理、渲染、动画、状态转换）的能力，且难以区分真正故障与非常规但有效的游戏事件。

Method: 1) 构建VideoGlitchBench基准数据集，包含5,238个来自120款游戏的视频，带有详细故障描述和精确时间跨度标注；2) 提出GliDe智能框架，包含三个关键组件：游戏感知上下文记忆用于信息推理、辩论式反射器进行多视角故障检测与验证、事件级定位模块从碎片化时间证据恢复完整故障间隔；3) 设计联合评估协议衡量语义保真度和时间精度。

Result: 当前多模态模型在此任务上仍面临巨大挑战，而GliDe相比对应的基线模型实现了显著更强的性能。

Conclusion: 开放域视频游戏故障检测是一个具有重要研究价值的新任务，VideoGlitchBench和GliDe为该领域提供了有效的基准和解决方案，但仍需进一步研究以提升模型的故障理解与定位能力。

Abstract: Open-ended video game glitch detection aims to identify glitches in gameplay videos, describe them in natural language, and localize when they occur. Unlike conventional game glitch understanding tasks which have largely been framed as image-level recognition or closed-form question answering, this task requires reasoning about game-specific dynamics such as mechanics, physics, rendering, animation, and expected state transitions directly over continuous gameplay videos and distinguishing true glitches from unusual but valid in-game events. To support this task, we introduce VideoGlitchBench, the first benchmark for open-ended video game glitch detection with temporal localization. VideoGlitchBench contains 5,238 gameplay videos from 120 games, each annotated with detailed glitch descriptions and precise temporal spans, enabling unified evaluation of semantic understanding and temporal grounding. We further propose GliDe, an agentic framework with three key components: a game-aware contextual memory for informed reasoning, a debate-based reflector for multi-perspective glitch detection and verification, and an event-level grounding module that recovers complete glitch intervals from fragmented temporal evidence. We also design a task-specific evaluation protocol that jointly measures semantic fidelity and temporal accuracy. Experiments show that this task remains highly challenging for current multimodal models, while GliDe achieves substantially stronger performance than corresponding vanilla model baselines.

</details>


### [204] [More Capable, Less Cooperative? When LLMs Fail At Zero-Cost Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07821)
*Advait Yadav,Sid Black,Oliver Sourbut*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究探讨了大语言模型多智能体系统中的合作失败问题。在零摩擦环境（帮助他人既不损害也不受益助手自身）中，即使给予明确的合作指令，更强的模型（o3）反而合作率更低（仅17%最优集体表现），而较弱模型（o3-mini）达到50%。通过因果分解和推理分析，研究发现扩展智能本身无法解决多智能体协调问题，需要刻意设计合作机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在多智能体系统中日益协调，但尚不清楚合作失败发生的原因和位置。在现实世界的协调问题中（如组织知识共享、代码文档编写），帮助他人通常个人成本极低却能产生显著集体收益。然而，当帮助既不损害也不利益助手自身时，LLM是否会合作仍属未知。

Method: 构建多智能体实验设置，在零摩擦环境中研究合作行为，消除所有策略复杂性。通过自动化一侧智能体通信进行因果分解，将合作失败与能力失败分离，通过智能体推理分析追溯失败根源。测试针对性干预措施（显式协议和微小分享激励）。

Result: 能力无法预测合作行为：OpenAI o3仅达到最优集体表现的17%，而o3-mini达到50%，尽管两者接收完全相同的最大化群体收益指令。显式协议使低能力模型性能翻倍；微小分享激励改善了合作能力较弱的模型。

Conclusion: 仅扩展智能无法解决多智能体系统中的协调问题，即使帮助他人无需成本，仍需刻意设计合作机制。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly coordinate in multi-agent systems, yet we lack an understanding of where and why cooperation failures may arise. In many real-world coordination problems, from knowledge sharing in organizations to code documentation, helping others carries negligible personal cost while generating substantial collective benefits. However, whether LLM agents cooperate when helping neither benefits nor harms the helper, while being given explicit instructions to do so, remains unknown. We build a multi-agent setup designed to study cooperative behavior in a frictionless environment, removing all strategic complexity from cooperation. We find that capability does not predict cooperation: OpenAI o3 achieves only 17% of optimal collective performance while OpenAI o3-mini reaches 50%, despite identical instructions to maximize group revenue. Through a causal decomposition that automates one side of agent communication, we separate cooperation failures from competence failures, tracing their origins through agent reasoning analysis. Testing targeted interventions, we find that explicit protocols double performance for low-competence models, and tiny sharing incentives improve models with weak cooperation. Our findings suggest that scaling intelligence alone will not solve coordination problems in multi-agent systems and will require deliberate cooperative design, even when helping others costs nothing.

</details>


### [205] [Dynamic Attentional Context Scoping: Agent-Triggered Focus Sessions for Isolated Per-Agent Steering in Multi-Agent LLM Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07911)
*Nickson Patel*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: DACS通过双模式上下文管理机制解决多智能体LLM系统中的上下文污染问题，在200次试验中实现90-98.4%的 steering 准确率（基线仅21-60%），上下文效率提升3.53倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM编排系统中，N个并发智能体竞争上下文窗口时，各自的任务状态、部分输出和待处理问题会相互污染，导致决策质量下降。现有方法需依赖上下文压缩或检索，治标不治本。

Method: 提出动态注意力上下文作用域（DACS），采用两种非对称模式：Registry模式仅存储轻量级状态摘要（≤200 tokens）；Focus(a_i)模式注入目标智能体完整上下文同时压缩其他智能体至注册表条目。上下文隔离由智能体触发、非对称且确定性。

Result: 4阶段200次试验结果：DACS在8个合成场景中steering准确率达90.0-98.4%（基线21.0-60.0%，p<0.0001）；错误智能体污染从28-57%降至0-14%；上下文效率比达3.53x；准确率优势随N和D增长；N=3时提升17.2pp（p=0.0023），N=5时提升20.4pp（p=0.0008）。

Conclusion: DACS通过确定性上下文隔离有效消除跨智能体污染，无需压缩或检索即实现高效上下文管理。该方案在扩展性和复杂决策场景中优势显著，LLM-as-judge验证关键词匹配一致性（κ=0.909）。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM orchestration systems suffer from context pollution: when N concurrent agents compete for the orchestrator's context window, each agent's task state, partial outputs, and pending questions contaminate the steering interactions of every other agent, degrading decision quality. We introduce Dynamic Attentional Context Scoping (DACS), a mechanism in which the orchestrator operates in two asymmetric modes. In Registry mode it holds only lightweight per-agent status summaries (<=200 tokens each), remaining responsive to all agents and the user. When an agent emits a SteeringRequest, the orchestrator enters Focus(a_i) mode, injecting the full context of agent a_i while compressing all other agents to their registry entries. Context isolation is agent-triggered, asymmetric, and deterministic: the context window contains exactly F(a_i) + R_{-i} during steering, eliminating cross-agent contamination without requiring context compression or retrieval. We evaluate DACS across four experimental phases totalling 200 trials: Phase 1 tests N in {3,5,10} (60 trials); Phase 2 tests agent heterogeneity and adversarial dependencies (60 trials); Phase 3 tests decision density up to D=15 (40 trials); Phase 4 uses autonomous LLM agents for free-form questions (40 trials, Claude Haiku 4.5). Across all 8 synthetic scenarios, DACS achieves 90.0--98.4% steering accuracy versus 21.0--60.0% for a flat-context baseline (p < 0.0001 throughout), with wrong-agent contamination falling from 28--57% to 0--14% and context efficiency ratios of up to 3.53x. The accuracy advantage grows with N and D; keyword matching is validated by LLM-as-judge across all phases (mean kappa=0.909). DACS outperforms the flat-context baseline by +17.2pp at N=3 (p=0.0023) and +20.4pp at N=5 (p=0.0008) in Phase 4, with the advantage growing with N confirmed by two independent judges.

</details>


### [206] ["Theater of Mind" for LLMs: A Cognitive Architecture Based on Global Workspace Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08206)
*Wenlong Shang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出Global Workspace Agents (GWA)，一种受全局工作空间理论启发的认知架构，旨在解决当前大语言模型多智能体系统中因被动数据结构和静态记忆导致的认知停滞与同质化死锁问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM作为被动响应系统，缺乏时间连续性；多智能体框架依赖静态记忆池和被动消息传递，导致长时间执行中出现认知停滞和同质化死锁，制约了自主AI的实现。

Method: 1) 建立中央广播枢纽配合异构功能约束智能体群；2) 引入基于熵的内在驱动机制，量化语义多样性，动态调节生成温度以打破推理死锁；3) 采用双层记忆分叉策略实现长期认知连续性。

Result: 形成从被动数据结构到主动事件驱动离散动力系统的范式转变，构建可持续、自我导向的LLM智能体工程框架。

Conclusion: GWA提供了一种稳健、可复现的工程框架，通过认知周期持续性、自主死锁打破机制和长期记忆连续性，为构建真正自主的LLM智能体奠定基础。

Abstract: Modern Large Language Models (LLMs) operate fundamentally as Bounded-Input Bounded-Output (BIBO) systems. They remain in a passive state until explicitly prompted, computing localized responses without intrinsic temporal continuity. While effective for isolated tasks, this reactive paradigm presents a critical bottleneck for engineering autonomous artificial intelligence. Current multi-agent frameworks attempt to distribute cognitive load but frequently rely on static memory pools and passive message passing, which inevitably leads to cognitive stagnation and homogeneous deadlocks during extended execution. To address this structural limitation, we propose Global Workspace Agents (GWA), a cognitive architecture inspired by Global Workspace Theory. GWA transitions multi-agent coordination from a passive data structure to an active, event-driven discrete dynamical system. By coupling a central broadcast hub with a heterogeneous swarm of functionally constrained agents, the system maintains a continuous cognitive cycle. Furthermore, we introduce an entropy-based intrinsic drive mechanism that mathematically quantifies semantic diversity, dynamically regulating generation temperature to autonomously break reasoning deadlocks. Coupled with a dual-layer memory bifurcation strategy to ensure long-term cognitive continuity, GWA provides a robust, reproducible engineering framework for sustained, self-directed LLM agency.

</details>


### [207] [MemCoT: Test-Time Scaling through Memory-Driven Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08216)
*Haodong Lei,Junming Liu,Yirong Chen,Ding Wang,Hongsong Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: MemCoT通过将长上下文推理重新定义为迭代式、有状态的信息搜索过程来解决LLM在因果推理中的幻觉和遗忘问题。该框架引入多视图长期记忆感知模块和任务条件双短期记忆系统，在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在处理大规模碎片化长上下文时存在严重幻觉和灾难性遗忘问题。现有记忆机制采用静态、单步被动匹配范式，导致语义稀释和上下文碎片化。

Method: MemCoT提出两个核心组件：(1) 多视图长期记忆感知模块支持"放大"证据定位和"缩小"上下文扩展，识别相关证据位置并重建周围因果结构；(2) 任务条件双短期记忆系统（语义状态记忆+情景轨迹记忆）记录历史搜索决策，动态指导查询分解和剪枝。

Result: MemCoT在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S基准上实现SOTA性能，多个开源和闭源模型借助MemCoT提升表现。

Conclusion: MemCoT成功将长上下文推理从静态检索转变为迭代状态化搜索，有效缓解语义稀释和上下文碎片化问题。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) still suffer from severe hallucinations and catastrophic forgetting during causal reasoning over massive, fragmented long contexts. Existing memory mechanisms typically treat retrieval as a static, single-step passive matching process, leading to severe semantic dilution and contextual fragmentation. To overcome these fundamental bottlenecks, we propose MemCoT, a test-time memory scaling framework that redefines the reasoning process by transforming long-context reasoning into an iterative, stateful information search. MemCoT introduces a multi-view long-term memory perception module that enables Zoom-In evidence localization and Zoom-Out contextual expansion, allowing the model to first identify where relevant evidence resides and then reconstruct the surrounding causal structure necessary for reasoning. In addition, MemCoT employs a task-conditioned dual short-term memory system composed of semantic state memory and episodic trajectory memory. This short-term memory records historical search decisions and dynamically guides query decomposition and pruning across iterations. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that MemCoT establishes a state-of-the-art performance. Empowered by MemCoT, several open- and closed-source models achieve SOTA performance on the LoCoMo benchmark and LongMemEval-S benchmark.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [208] [Beyond Single Reports: Evaluating Automated ATT&CK Technique Extraction in Multi-Report Campaign Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07470)
*Md Nazmul Haque,Sivana Hamer,Brandon Wroblewski,Md Rayhanur Rahman,Laurie Williams*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究评估了29种ATT&CK攻击技术提取方法在多报告场景下的表现，发现多报告聚合相比单报告分析可提升F1分数约26%，但最高F1仅78.6%，且33.3%的误分类涉及语义相近的技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化CTI报告分析仅关注单报告场景，无法形成完整的攻击活动视图，亟需多报告聚合方法。

Method: 实证评估29种方法（NER、encoder分类、decoder LLM），覆盖90份CTI报告和三大攻击活动（SolarWinds、XZ Utils、Log4j）。

Result: 多报告聚合提升F1约26%，性能在5-15份报告后饱和；SolarWinds最高F1达78.6%，XZ Utils仅54.9%；33.3%误分类源于语义相似技术。

Conclusion: 多报告聚合显著改善技术提取，但提取性能仍有限，语义相似技术的误分类严重影响控制覆盖。

Abstract: Large-scale cyberattacks, referred to as campaigns, are documented across multiple CTI reports from diverse sources, with some providing a high-level overview of attack techniques and others providing technical details. Extracting attack techniques from reports is essential for organizations to identify the controls required to protect against attacks. Manually extracting techniques at scale is impractical. Existing automated methods focus on single reports, leaving many attack techniques and their controls undetected, resulting in a fragmented view of campaign behavior. The goal of this study is to aid security researchers in extracting attack techniques and controls from a campaign by replicating and comparing the performance of the state-of-the-art ATT&CK technique extraction methods in a multi-report campaign setting compared to prior single-report evaluations. We conduct an empirical study of 29 methods to extract attack techniques, spanning named entity recognition (NER), encoder-based classification, and decoder-based LLM approaches. Our study analyzes 90 CTI reports across three major attack campaigns: SolarWinds, XZ Utils, and Log4j, using both quantitative performance metrics and their impact on controls. Our results show that aggregating multiple CTI reports improves the F1 score by about 26% over single-report analysis, with most approaches reaching performance saturation after 5--15 reports. Despite these gains, extraction performance remains limited, with maximum F1 scores of 78.6% for SolarWinds and 54.9% for XZ Utils. Moreover, up to 33.3% of misclassifications involve semantically similar techniques that share tactics and overlap in descriptions. The misclassification has a disproportionate effect on control coverage. Reports that are longer and include technical details consistently perform better, even though their readability scores are low.

</details>


### [209] [Beyond Human-Readable: Rethinking Software Engineering Conventions for the Agentic Development Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07502)
*Dmytro Ustynov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在AI智能体时代，软件工程原则需要从面向人类开发者转向面向AI智能体。作者提出"语义密度优化"原则，并通过日志格式实验证明：激进压缩反而使总成本增加67%，因为它将解释负担转移到模型的推理阶段。文章进一步提出程序骨架概念，倡导将语义意图与人类可读表示解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六十年来的软件工程原则一直以人类开发者为中心，但LLM智能体的出现带来了一个具有根本不同约束的新"消费者"。现有实践假设最终理解者是人类，而AI智能体在读取、编写、导航和调试代码库时可能有完全不同的需求，这促使作者重新审视传统软件工程约定的合理性。

Method: 作者设计了一项受控实验，在四种日志格式条件下（人类可读、结构化、压缩、工具辅助压缩）测试日志格式的标记经济性。通过改变日志格式的表示方式，观察输入标记减少与总会话成本之间的关系。

Result: 实验发现一个反直觉的结果：激进压缩虽然将输入标记减少17%，但总会话成本反而增加了67%。原因是压缩将解释负担转移到了模型的推理阶段，说明并非所有"冗余"信息都是无价值的，某些人类友好格式可能对AI推理更高效。

Conclusion: 作者提出语义密度优化原则——消除零信息标记，同时保留高语义价值标记。进一步提出"程序骨架"概念用于智能体代码导航，并主张将语义意图与人类可读表示进行根本性解耦。这代表软件工程在AI智能体时代的新方向。

Abstract: For six decades, software engineering principles have been optimized for a single consumer: the human developer. The rise of agentic AI development, where LLM-based agents autonomously read, write, navigate, and debug codebases, introduces a new primary consumer with fundamentally different constraints. This paper presents a systematic analysis of human-centric conventions under agentic pressure and proposes a key design principle: semantic density optimization, eliminating tokens that carry zero information while preserving tokens that carry high semantic value. We validate this principle through a controlled experiment on log format token economy across four conditions (human-readable, structured, compressed, and tool-assisted compressed), demonstrating a counterintuitive finding: aggressive compression increased total session cost by 67% despite reducing input tokens by 17%, because it shifted interpretive burden to the model's reasoning phase. We extend this principle to propose the rehabilitation of classical anti-patterns, introduce the program skeleton concept for agentic code navigation, and argue for a fundamental decoupling of semantic intent from human-readable representation.

</details>


### [210] [Program Analysis Guided LLM Agent for Proof-of-Concept Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07624)
*Achintya Desai,Md Shafiuzzaman,Wenbo Guo,Tevfik Bultan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PAGENT是一种结合轻量级静态分析和动态分析指导的混合框架，显著提升漏洞PoC自动生成的成功率，相比现有最佳智能体方法提升132%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件安全分析中，开发者在收到漏洞报告后需要可靠地复现漏洞，但现有自动化方法（符号执行、模糊测试、LLM生成）存在成功率低、需要专家指导、可扩展性差等问题。

Method: PAGENT采用混合方法：(1) 轻量级规则驱动的静态分析阶段提供静态指导；(2) 基于sanitizer的profiling和覆盖率信息提供动态分析指导；(3) 与PoC生成智能体集成，形成完整的生成管道。

Result: 实验表明，PAGENT在PoC生成任务上比之前的最佳智能体方法提升132%。

Conclusion: 结合静态分析与动态分析指导的混合方法能有效提升自动化漏洞PoC生成的成功率和可扩展性，为自动化漏洞验证提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Software developers frequently receive vulnerability reports that require them to reproduce the vulnerability in a reliable manner by generating a proof-of-concept (PoC) input that triggers it. Given the source code for a software project and a specific code location for a potential vulnerability, automatically generating a PoC for the given vulnerability has been a challenging research problem. Symbolic execution and fuzzing techniques require expert guidance and manual steps and face scalability challenges for PoC generation. Although recent advances in LLMs have increased the level of automation and scalability, the success rate of PoC generation with LLMs remains quite low. In this paper, we present a novel approach called Program Analysis Guided proof of concept generation agENT (PAGENT) that is scalable and significantly improves the success rate of automated PoC generation compared to prior results. PAGENT integrates lightweight and rule-based static analysis phases for providing static analysis guidance and sanitizer-based profiling and coverage information for providing dynamic analysis guidance with a PoC generation agent. Our experiments demonstrate that the resulting hybrid approach significantly outperforms the prior top-performing agentic approach by 132% for the PoC generation task.

</details>


### [211] [MVOS_HSI: A Python Library for Preprocessing Agricultural Crop Hyperspectral Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07656)
*Rishik Aggarwal,Krisha Joshi,Pappu Kumar Yadav,Jianwei Qin,Thomas F. Burks,Moon S. Kim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Hyperspectral imaging (HSI) allows researchers to study plant traits non-destructively. By capturing hundreds of narrow spectral bands per pixel, it reveals details about plant biochemistry and stress that standard cameras miss. However, processing this data is often challenging. Many labs still rely on loosely organized collections of lab-specific MATLAB or Python scripts, which makes workflows difficult to share and results difficult to reproduce. MVOS_HSI is an open-source Python library that provides an end-to-end workflow for processing leaf-level HSI data. The software handles everything from calibrating raw ENVI files to detecting and clipping individual leaves based on multiple vegetation indices (NDVI, CIRedEdge and GCI). It also includes tools for data augmentation to create training-time variations for machine learning and utilities to visualize spectral profiles. MVOS_HSI can be used as an importable Python library or run directly from the command line. The code and documentation are available on GitHub. By consolidating these common tasks into a single package, MVOS_HSI helps researchers produce consistent and reproducible results in plant phenotyping

</details>


### [212] [MIMIC-Py: An Extensible Tool for Personality-Driven Automated Game Testing with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07752)
*Yifei Chen,Sarra Habchi,Lili Wei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MIMIC-Py是一个基于Python的自动化游戏测试工具，旨在将人格驱动的LLM智能体转化为可复用、可扩展的框架。该工具将人格特质作为可配置输入，采用模块化架构，支持多种交互机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视频游戏是复杂的非确定性系统，难以大规模自动测试。虽然先前研究表明人格驱动的LLM智能体可以提升行为多样性和测试覆盖率，但现有工具多为研究原型，缺乏跨游戏复用能力。

Method: MIMIC-Py采用模块化架构，将规划、执行和记忆模块与游戏特定逻辑解耦；将人格特质暴露为可配置输入；支持多种交互机制（通过API或合成代码与游戏交互）。

Result: 该工具实现了向新游戏环境的最少工程量部署，弥合了研究原型与实际自动化游戏测试之间的差距。

Conclusion: MIMIC-Py为构建实用化的游戏自动化测试工具提供了可复用、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern video games are complex, non-deterministic systems that are difficult to test automatically at scale. Although prior work shows that personality-driven Large Language Model (LLM) agents can improve behavioural diversity and test coverage, existing tools largely remain research prototypes and lack cross-game reusability.
  This tool paper presents MIMIC-Py, a Python-based automated game-testing tool that transforms personality-driven LLM agents into a reusable and extensible framework. MIMIC-Py exposes personality traits as configurable inputs and adopts a modular architecture that decouples planning, execution, and memory from game-specific logic. It supports multiple interaction mechanisms, enabling agents to interact with games via exposed APIs or synthesized code. We describe the design of MIMIC-Py and show how it enables deployment to new game environments with minimal engineering effort, bridging the gap between research prototypes and practical automated game testing.
  The source code and a demo video are available on our project webpage: https://mimic-persona.github.io/MIMIC-Py-Home-Page/.

</details>


### [213] [An Empirical Study on Influence-Based Pretraining Data Selection for Code Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07769)
*Chengli Xing,Zhengran Zeng,Gexiang Fang,Rui Xie,Wei Ye,Shikun Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探索了数据影响分数过滤方法在代码大语言模型预训练数据筛选中的有效性。研究发现，基于验证集损失的数据影响分数过滤可以提升模型的编程性能，且有益训练数据的筛选标准因下游编程任务不同而存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练数据过滤研究主要聚焦于通用数据集，对编程数据集的关注不足。代码大语言模型（Code-LLMs）的快速发展凸显了高质量代码预训练数据的重要性，但缺乏针对编程数据集的过滤方法研究。

Method: 1. 提出针对生成式编程任务的数据影响分数计算方法：将多种下游编码任务转化为验证集，以模型在验证集上的损失作为性能指标；2. 从零开始预训练1B参数的Code-LLM，使用1000亿代码token数据集；3. 开展广泛实证研究，评估数据影响分数过滤方法的效果，包括：过滤技术对模型性能的提升效果、有益训练数据特征在不同训练阶段和编程任务中的变化、以及基于预测的数据影响分数过滤方法的可行性。

Result: 数据影响分数过滤（基于验证集损失）能够提升模型的编程性能。有益训练数据的筛选标准在不同下游编程任务间存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 数据影响分数过滤方法可有效应用于代码预训练数据的筛选，但需针对不同编程任务定制筛选策略。研究为Code-LLMs的高质量代码数据筛选提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Recent advancements in code large language models (Code-LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in resolving programming related tasks. Meanwhile, researchers have recognized that the quality of pre-training data is crucial for improving LLM performance. However, most of the existing research on pre-training data filtering has focused on general datasets, and little attention for programming datasets. In this paper, we aim to address this gap by exploring the effectiveness of a widely used general data filtering technique, i.e., data-influence-score filtering, within the context of programming-related datasets. To this end, we first introduce a method for calculating data-influence-score for generative programming tasks which involves transforming a variety of downstream coding tasks into validation sets and using the models loss on these sets as a performance metric. Next, we pre-train a Code-LLMs with 1 billion parameters from scratch on a dataset of 100 billion code tokens. Based on it, we conduct an extensive empirical study to evaluate the effectiveness of data-influence-score filtering methods. Specifically, we examine how well this technique improves model performance, investigate how the characteristics of beneficial training data vary across different training stages and programming tasks, and assess the feasibility of prediction-based data-influence-score filtering method. Our findings show that data-influence-score filtering based on validation-set-loss can enhance models programming performance. Moreover, we observe that the criteria of beneficial training data differ significantly across various downstream programming tasks.

</details>


### [214] [To Copilot and Beyond: 22 AI Systems Developers Want Built](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07830)
*Rudrajit Choudhuri,Christian Bird,Carmen Badea,Anita Sarma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过调查860名微软开发者，研究AI工具在实际开发工作中的需求分布。研究发现开发者希望AI处理的并非核心编码工作，而是围绕核心工作的"组装任务"。论文定义了"有限授权"(bounded delegation)这一概念，指出AI工具的价值不仅在于做什么，更在于何时停止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI开发工具主要聚焦于代码生成这一环节（约占开发者10%的工作时间），而忽视了开发者在其他90%时间的需求，如代码审查、需求分析、集成调试等。研究者试图系统性地识别开发者真正需要的AI系统类型。

Method: 采用多模型委员会(multi-model council)进行主题分析，结合人工审核(human-in-the-loop)，对860名微软开发者进行问卷调查，归纳出22种开发者期望的AI系统，分类为5个任务类别。

Result: 识别出开发者希望的22种AI系统，覆盖5个任务类别。核心发现是"右移负担"(right-shift burden)现象：开发者希望质量信号嵌入工作流更早阶段；同时提出"有限授权"模式：开发者希望AI承担组装性工作，而非专业性核心工作。

Conclusion: AI辅助开发工具的价值在于边界管理——开发者需要AI处理事务性工作，但保持对核心专业的控制权。AI工具的设计应尊重开发者的职业身份定位，在合适边界处停止介入。

Abstract: Developers spend roughly one-tenth of their workday writing code, yet most AI tooling targets that fraction. This paper asks what should be built for the rest. We surveyed 860 Microsoft developers to understand where they want AI support, and where they want it to stay out. Using a human-in-the-loop, multi-model council-based thematic analysis, we identify 22 AI systems that developers want built across five task categories. For each, we describe the problem it solves, what makes it hard to build, and the constraints developers place on its behavior. Our findings point to a growing right-shift burden in AI-assisted development: developers wanted systems that embed quality signals earlier in their workflow to keep pace with accelerating code generation, while enforcing explicit authority scoping, provenance, uncertainty signaling, and least-privilege access throughout. This tension reveals a pattern we call "bounded delegation": developers wanted AI to absorb the assembly work surrounding their craft, never the craft itself. That boundary tracks where they locate professional identity, suggesting that the value of AI tooling may lie as much in where and how precisely it stops as in what it does.

</details>


### [215] [ZeroCoder: Can LLMs Improve Code Generation Without Ground-Truth Supervision?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07864)
*Lishui Fan,Mouxiang Chen,Tingwei Zhu,Kui Liu,Xin Xia,Shanping Li,Zhongxin Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ZeroCoder提出了一个无标签的代码-测试协同进化框架，通过代码生成器和测试生成器的互相反馈来提升代码生成质量，无需人工标注的监督信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RLVR（带可验证奖励的强化学习）流程依赖人工设计的测试用例，导致进展受限于稀缺且昂贵的标注成本。现有尝试使用自生成测试的工作因模型测试生成能力不足、缺乏判别性测试而效果受限。

Method: ZeroCoder提出代码-测试协同进化框架：(1)对每个问题执行采样的解决方案与测试用例生成通过矩阵；(2)通过可插拔的选择算法识别可能正确的共识解决方案子集和一致的测试；(3)推导出角色特定奖励。此外引入基于排序的预过滤训练测试生成器，使用课程学习平衡有效性和变异驱动的判别性；并识别选择器漂移问题，提出DyB4贝叶斯选择器，仅用10个标注样本动态重新校准先验。

Result: 在三个模型和六个基准测试上，ZeroCoder持续改进代码和测试生成。在完全无标签设置下，Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct上代码生成提升14.5%；结合DyB4后提升达21.6%，测试生成提升24.3%，接近神谕监督性能。

Conclusion: ZeroCoder成功实现了代码和测试生成的协同进化，在无人工标注的情况下显著提升代码生成质量，DyB4选择器有效解决了协同进化过程中的选择器漂移问题，为无监督代码生成提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Code generation is important in software engineering, and Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) is a powerful paradigm to improve it through execution-based feedback. However, most RLVR pipelines rely on human-curated tests, making progress bottlenecked by scarce and costly supervision. Existing work tried to use self-generated tests to ground rewards, but the lack of discriminative tests constrains the effect due to the sub-optimal performance of the model on test generation. We aim to improve code generation without ground-truth supervision by co-evolving code and test generation, so that their interactions yield progressively more informative supervision. To this end, we present ZeroCoder, a fully label-free co-evolutionary framework that jointly trains a Coder and a Tester using execution feedback from self-generated code-test interactions. For each problem, ZeroCoder executes sampled solutions against sampled tests to form a passing matrix, identifies a consensus subset of likely-correct solutions and consistent tests via a pluggable selection algorithm, and derives role-specific rewards. To ensure reward quality, ZeroCoder filters low-information instances via rank-based pre-filtering and trains the Tester with a curriculum balancing validity and mutation-driven discriminativeness. We further identify selector drift, the progressive miscalibration of fixed selection rules during co-evolution, and introduce DyB4, a Bayesian selector that uses as few as 10 labeled instances to recalibrate its priors dynamically. Across three models and six benchmarks, ZeroCoder consistently improves code generation and test generation. In the fully label-free setting, it improves code generation by up to 14.5% over the base model on Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct. With DyB4, the gain reaches 21.6%, while test generation improves by 24.3%, approaching oracle-supervised performance.

</details>


### [216] [Investigating Code Reuse in Software Redesign: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07919)
*Xiaowen Zhang,Huaien Zhang,Shin Hwei Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过行动研究方法对Soot/SootUp重构案例进行双向研究，发现重设计中存在非线性迁移、TODO延迟复用、测试移植被忽视以及bug传播等问题。核心挑战是追踪对应代码和测试，提出利用克隆检测推导线束关系，并通过语义对齐启发式方法实现可扩展层级检测策略，在两个重构项目对中验证效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件重构在保持功能的同时提升质量属性，但在跨仓库重构场景下，手动复用代码和测试成本高且易出错。静态分析工具领域对跨仓库重设计需求尤为迫切，但现有方法缺乏系统性指导。

Method: 采用行动研究方法，将实证调查与已验证的开源贡献相结合，对Soot/SootUp重构案例进行双向研究。通过克隆检测逆向推导原始项目与重构项目之间的代码映射关系，结合研究中提取的语义复用模式，提出语义对齐启发式方法（Semantic Alignment Heuristics）及可扩展的层级检测策略。

Result: 在Soot/SootUp和FindBugs/SpotBugs两个重构项目对上进行评估，在SAS阈值为0.5条件下，无关克隆平均减少33-99%，在1,749个样本基准上精确率提升达86%。同时为重构项目贡献了5个issue和10个pull request，其中8个已合并。

Conclusion: 追踪对应代码和测试是跨仓库重设计的关键挑战。语义对齐启发式方法结合层级检测策略能有效识别代码对应关系，显著减少无关克隆并提升精确率。该方法具有良好的可扩展性，已在实际开源项目中得到验证和应用。

Abstract: Software redesign preserves functionality while improving quality attributes, but manual reuse of code and tests is costly and error-prone, especially in crossrepository redesigns. Focusing on static analyzers where cross-repo redesign needs often arise, we conduct a bidirectional study of the ongoing Soot/SootUp redesign case using an action research methodology that combines empirical investigation with validated open-source contributions. Our study reveals: (1) non-linear migration which necessitates bidirectional reuse, (2) deferred reuse via TODOs, (3) neglected test porting, and (4) residual bug propagation during migrations. We identify tracking corresponding code and tests as the key challenge, and address it by retrofitting clone detection to derive code mappings between original and redesigned projects. Guided by semantic reuse patterns derived in our study, we propose Semantic Alignment Heuristics and a scalable hierarchical detection strategy. Evaluations on two redesigned project pairs (Soot/SootUp and FindBugs/SpotBugs) show that our approach achieves an average reduction of 33-99% in likely irrelevant clones at a SAS threshold of 0.5 across all tool results, and improves precision up to 86% on our benchmark of 1,749 samples. Moreover, we contribute to the redesigned projects by submitting five issues and 10 pull requests, of which eight have been merged.

</details>


### [217] [Log-based, Business-aware REST API Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08007)
*Ding Yang,Ruixiang Qian,Zhao Wei,Zhenyu Chen,Chunrong Fang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LoBREST通过分析历史请求日志(HRLogs)来测试REST API的业务敏感功能，使用局部切片策略划分日志，通过增强缺失操作和资源来完成切片，最后使用这些增强切片作为种子进行业务感知模糊测试。在17个真实服务上优于8个工具，在16个服务上达到最高操作覆盖率，在15个服务上达到最高行覆盖率，平均提升2.1倍和1.2倍，检测到108个5XX bug。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于REST规范(如Swagger/OpenAPI)的测试技术仅能提取资源约束，无法充分测试复杂业务敏感功能。这些功能需要额外的业务约束，而这些约束通常不在REST规范中，导致现有技术测试不充分。

Method: 1) 使用局部切片策略将HRLogs划分为更小的切片以获得紧凑的操作序列；2) 增强切片：(a)为HRLogs中缺失的操作添加切片，(b)补全切片中缺失的资源；3) 使用增强后的切片作为初始种子进行业务感知模糊测试以提高测试充分性。

Result: LoBREST在17个真实服务上优于8个工具(包括Arat-rl、Morest和Deeprest)。在16个服务上达到最高操作覆盖率，在15个服务上达到最高行覆盖率，平均分别提升2.1倍和1.2倍。检测到108个5XX bug，其中38个未被其他工具发现。

Conclusion: 基于历史日志的业务感知测试方法能有效弥补传统规范驱动测试的不足，显著提升复杂业务功能的测试覆盖率和bug检测能力。

Abstract: REST APIs enable collaboration among microservices. A single fault in a REST API can bring down the entire microservice system and cause significant financial losses, underscoring the importance of REST API testing. Effectively testing REST APIs requires thoroughly exercising the functionalities behind them. To this end, existing techniques leverage REST specifications (e.g., Swagger or OpenAPI) to generate test cases. Using the resource constraints extracted from specifications, these techniques work well for testing simple, business-insensitive functionalities, such as resource creation, retrieval, update, and deletion. However, for complex, business-sensitive functionalities, these specification-based techniques often fall short, since exercising such functionalities requires additional business constraints that are typically absent from REST specifications. In this paper, we present LoBREST, a log-based, business-aware REST API testing technique that leverages historical request logs (HRLogs) to effectively exercise the business-sensitive functionalities behind REST APIs. To obtain compact operation sequences that preserve clean and complete business constraints, LoBREST first employs a locality-slicing strategy to partition HRLogs into smaller slices. Then, to ensure the effectiveness of the obtained slices, LoBREST enhances them in two steps: (1) adding slices for operations missing from HRLogs, and (2) completing missing resources within the slices. Finally, to improve test adequacy, LoBREST uses these enhanced slices as initial seeds to perform business-aware fuzzing. LoBREST outperformed eight tools (including Arat-rl, Morest, and Deeprest) across 17 real-world services. It achieved top operation coverage on 16 services and line coverage on 15, averaging 2.1x and 1.2x improvements over the runner-up. LoBREST detected 108 5XX bugs, including 38 found by no other tool.

</details>


### [218] [Externalization in LLM Agents: A Unified Review of Memory, Skills, Protocols and Harness Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08224)
*Chenyu Zhou,Huacan Chai,Wenteng Chen,Zihan Guo,Rong Shan,Yuanyi Song,Tianyi Xu,Yingxuan Yang,Aofan Yu,Weiming Zhang,Congming Zheng,Jiachen Zhu,Zeyu Zheng,Zhuosheng Zhang,Xingyu Lou,Changwang Zhang,Zhihui Fu,Jun Wang,Weiwen Liu,Jianghao Lin,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个系统层面的分析框架，认为当前LLM智能体的发展不仅依赖于更强的模型本身，更依赖于更优的外部认知基础设施。作者将这种从模型权重到上下文再到外部架构的演进称为"外部化"(externalization)，并认为智能体基础设施通过将困难的认知负担转化为模型能更可靠解决的问题形式来实现其价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为LLM智能体能力的提升主要依赖于更强的模型权重，但实践中发现，许多之前期望模型内部恢复的能力正在被外部化到各种组件中。本文动机是解释这一现象并提供一个统一的理论框架来理解为什么"更好的外部基础设施"对智能体进展越来越重要。

Method: 本文采用概念性分析和方法：1) 借鉴认知Artifacts理论作为分析透镜；2) 追溯从权重到上下文再到外部架构的历史演进；3) 将外部化形式解耦为三种相互关联但又不同的类型：记忆（跨时间外部化状态）、技能（外部化程序性专业知识）、协议（外部化交互结构）；4) 分析"harness工程"作为协调这些组件的整合层；5) 识别新兴研究方向和开放挑战。

Result: 本文构建了一个系统级解释框架，核心论点为：智能体基础设施的价值不仅在于增加辅助组件，更在于将困难的认知负担转化为模型能更可靠解决的形式。记忆、技能、协议分别对应状态、程序专业知识、交互结构的外部化，而harness工程则将这些模块协调为受治理的执行系统。

Conclusion: 未来研究方向包括：自演进harness、共享智能体基础设施；开放挑战包括：评估体系、治理机制、模型与外部基础设施的长期协同演化问题。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly built less by changing model weights than by reorganizing the runtime around them. Capabilities that earlier systems expected the model to recover internally are now externalized into memory stores, reusable skills, interaction protocols, and the surrounding harness that makes these modules reliable in practice. This paper reviews that shift through the lens of externalization. Drawing on the idea of cognitive artifacts, we argue that agent infrastructure matters not merely because it adds auxiliary components, but because it transforms hard cognitive burdens into forms that the model can solve more reliably. Under this view, memory externalizes state across time, skills externalize procedural expertise, protocols externalize interaction structure, and harness engineering serves as the unification layer that coordinates them into governed execution. We trace a historical progression from weights to context to harness, analyze memory, skills, and protocols as three distinct but coupled forms of externalization, and examine how they interact inside a larger agent system. We further discuss the trade-off between parametric and externalized capability, identify emerging directions such as self-evolving harnesses and shared agent infrastructure, and discuss open challenges in evaluation, governance, and the long-term co-evolution of models and external infrastructure. The result is a systems-level framework for explaining why practical agent progress increasingly depends not only on stronger models, but on better external cognitive infrastructure.

</details>


### [219] [A Comparative Study of Semantic Log Representations for Software Log-based Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08028)
*Yuqing Wang,Ying Song,Xiaozhou Li,Nana Reinikainen,Mika V. Mäntylä*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 基准测试表明，在CPU环境下，基于BERT的日志表示方法有效性最高但效率较低，而静态词嵌入方法效率高但有效性不足。提出QTyBERT方法，通过轻量级量化BERT变体SysBE和多系统无监督学习的语义增强模块CroSysEh，实现与BERT相当的检测效果同时接近静态词嵌入的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度学习日志异常检测方法依赖语义日志表示方法，但不同表示方法的影响尚未被深入研究。在CPU部署环境下，存在有效性与效率之间的权衡问题：BERT方法效果最好但计算开销大，静态词嵌入效率高但效果不足。

Method: 在三个公开日志数据集（BGL、Thunderbird、Spirit）上，对比测试了静态词嵌入方法（Word2Vec、GloVe、FastText）与BERT上下文嵌入方法在不同深度学习模型上的表现。提出QTyBERT方法：使用SysBE（轻量级量化BERT变体）在CPU上高效生成日志向量嵌入，并通过CroSysEh模块利用多系统未标注日志进行无监督学习以增强语义表达能力。

Result: 实验证实了CPU环境下的有效性-效率权衡现象。QTyBERT生成的日志嵌入在深度学习模型上取得了与BERT相当或更好的检测效果，同时日志嵌入生成时间接近静态词嵌入方法。

Conclusion: QTyBERT通过系统特定量化和跨系统语义增强，有效解决了日志异常检测中语义表示方法的有效性与效率平衡问题，为CPU部署场景提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent deep learning (DL) methods for log anomaly detection increasingly rely on semantic log representation methods that convert the textual content of log events into vector embeddings as input to DL models. However, these DL methods are typically evaluated as end-to-end pipelines, while the impact of different semantic representation methods is not well understood. In this paper, we benchmark widely used semantic log representation methods, including static word embedding methods (Word2Vec, GloVe, and FastText) and the BERT-based contextual embedding method, across diverse DL models for log-event level anomaly detection on three publicly available log datasets: BGL, Thunderbird, and Spirit. We identify an effectiveness--efficiency trade off under CPU deployment settings: the BERT-based method is more effective, but incurs substantially longer log embedding generation time, limiting its practicality; static word embedding methods are efficient but are generally less effective and may yield insufficient detection performance. Motivated by this finding, we propose QTyBERT, a novel semantic log representation method that better balances this trade-off. QTyBERT uses SysBE, a lightweight BERT variant with system-specific quantization, to efficiently encode log events into vector embeddings on CPUs, and leverages CroSysEh to enhance the semantic expressiveness of these log embeddings. CroSysEh is trained unsupervisedly using unlabeled logs from multiple systems to capture the underlying semantic structure of the BERT model's embedding space. We evaluate QTyBERT against existing semantic log representation methods. Our results show that, for the DL models, using QTyBERT-generated log embeddings achieves detection effectiveness comparable to or better than BERT-generated log embeddings, while bringing log embedding generation time closer to that of static word embedding methods.

</details>


### [220] [Can LLMs Deobfuscate Binary Code? A Systematic Analysis of Large Language Models into Pseudocode Deobfuscation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08083)
*Li Hu,Xiuwei Shang,Jieke Shi,Shaoyin Cheng,Junqi Zhang,Gangyang Li,Zhou Yang,Weiming Zhang,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了BinDebfBench，首个评估LLM在二进制去混淆能力的综合基准测试，涵盖编译前、编译时和编译后各阶段的混淆变换。研究表明推理能力和领域专业知识比模型规模更重要，任务特定的微调优于广泛的领域预训练，推理模型在严重混淆下仍能保持鲁棒性并跨不同ISA泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混淆技术被广泛用于阻碍分析和隐藏程序逻辑，而现有研究缺乏对LLM二进制去混淆能力的系统性评估，需要一个标准化基准来衡量不同模型的有效性。

Method: 构建了BinDeObfBench基准测试，涵盖多种混淆变换阶段（编译前、编译时、编译后），对多种LLM进行系统评估，包括测试推理能力、领域专业知识、模型规模影响、微调策略、上下文学习效果等。

Result: 推理能力和领域专业知识比模型规模更影响去混淆效果；任务特定的监督微调优于广泛领域预训练；推理模型在严重混淆下保持鲁棒性，能跨不同ISA和优化级别泛化；上下文学习对标准模型有益但对推理模型效果有限。

Conclusion: 任务特定的微调和推理驱动的策略对二进制去混淆至关重要，BinDeObfBench可作为未来二进制去混淆研究的基准。

Abstract: Deobfuscating binary code remains a fundamental challenge in reverse engineering, as obfuscation is widely used to hinder analysis and conceal program logic. Although large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in recovering semantics from obfuscated binaries, a systematic evaluation of their effectiveness is still lacking. In this work, we present BinDeObfBench, the first comprehensive benchmark for assessing LLM-based binary deobfuscation across diverse transformations spanning pre-compilation, compile-time, and post-compilation stages. Our evaluation shows that deobfuscation performance depends more on reasoning capability and domain expertise than on model scale, and that task-specific supervised fine-tuning consistently outperforms broad domain pre-training. Reasoning models can maintain robustness under severe obfuscation, generalize across different instruction set architectures (ISAs) and optimization levels. In-context learning benefits standard models but yields limited gains for reasoning models. Overall, our study highlights the importance of task-specific fine-tuning and reasoning-driven strategies, and positions BinDeObfBench as a basis for future work in binary deobfuscation.

</details>


### [221] [GALA: Multimodal Graph Alignment for Bug Localization in Automated Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08089)
*Zhuoyao Liu,Zhengran Zeng,Shu-Dong Huang,Yang Liu,Shikun Zhang,Wei Ye*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: GALA是一个多模态自动程序修复框架，通过图对齐方法解决GUI截图与代码之间的跨模态定位问题。框架包含四阶段流程：构建UI图、文件级对齐、函数级对齐和补丁生成。在SWE-bench Multimodal基准上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based APR方法在处理多模态场景（GUI截图报告bug）时，将图像转为纯文本，丢失关键空间关系，导致视觉观察与代码组件严重脱节，定位退化为不精确的关键词匹配。

Method: GALA采用四阶段层次化结构对齐：(1)构建Image UI Graph捕获视觉元素及结构关系；(2)通过交叉引用UI图与仓库级结构进行文件级对齐，定位候选文件；(3)利用细粒度代码依赖（如调用图）进行函数级对齐，将视觉元素精确锚定到代码组件；(4)在已对齐的文件和函数上下文中生成补丁。

Result: 在SWE-bench Multimodal基准上，GALA实现了最先进的性能，验证了层次化结构对齐的有效性。

Conclusion: GALA通过在模态间系统性地强制语义和关系一致性，建立了高精度的视觉到代码映射，为多模态APR从隐式语义猜测转向显式结构推理提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based Automated Program Repair (APR) has shown strong potential on textual benchmarks, yet struggles in multimodal scenarios where bugs are reported with GUI screenshots. Existing methods typically convert images into plain text, which discards critical spatial relationships and causes a severe disconnect between visual observations and code components, leading localization to degrade into imprecise keyword matching. To bridge this gap, we propose GALA (Graph Alignment for Localization in APR), a framework that shifts multimodal APR from implicit semantic guessing to explicit structural reasoning. GALA operates in four stages: it first constructs an Image UI Graph to capture visual elements and their structural relationships; then performs file-level alignment by cross-referencing this UI graph with repository-level structures (e.g., file references) to locate candidate files; next conducts function-level alignment by reasoning over fine-grained code dependencies (e.g., call graphs) to precisely ground visual elements to corresponding code components; and finally performs patch generation within the grounded code context based on the aligned files and functions. By systematically enforcing both semantic and relational consistency across modalities, GALA establishes a highly accurate visual-to-code mapping. Evaluations on the SWE-bench Multimodal benchmark demonstrate that GALA achieves state-of-the-art performance, highlighting the effectiveness of hierarchical structural alignment.

</details>


### [222] [Test-Oriented Programming: rethinking coding for the GenAI era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08102)
*Jorge Melegati*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM的新编程范式——测试导向编程(TOP)，开发者只需编写基于自然语言规范的测试代码，由LLM负责生成生产代码，作者通过概念验证工具验证了该方案的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM辅助编程工具主要依赖改进的自动补全或多智能体协作，文章认为LLM能够支持更高层次的抽象，因此提出TOP范式，旨在改变开发者与代码生成的交互方式。

Method: 开发了一个概念验证(PoC)工具，使用两个不同的LLM生成一个小型的命令行程序来评估该方案的可行性。

Result: 取得了有前景的结果，同时识别出了该范式应用于实际项目时面临的挑战。

Conclusion: TOP是一种可行的编程新范式，但仍需解决若干挑战才能在真实项目中落地。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown astonishing capability of generating software code, leading to its use to support developers in programming. Proposed tools have relied either on assistants for improved auto-complete or multi-agents, in which different model instances are orchestrated to perform parts of a problem to reach a complete solution. We argue that LLMs can enable a higher-level of abstraction, a new paradigm we called Test-Oriented Programming (TOP). Within this paradigm, developers only have to check test code generated based on natural language specifications, rather than focusing on production code, which could be delegated to the LLMs. To evaluate the feasibility of this proposal, we developed a proof-of-concept tool and used it to generate a small command-line program employing two different LLMs. We obtained promising results and identified challenges for the use of this paradigm for real projects.

</details>


### [223] [Towards Improving the External Validity of Software Engineering Experiments with Transportability Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08200)
*Julian Frattini,Richard Torkar,Robert Feldt,Carlo Furia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出在软件工程研究中采用"Transportability方法"，通过结合观察性数据和实验性数据来增强实验的外部有效性，解决软件工程实验招募代表性样本困难的问题，并提供了应用指南和研究场景展望。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程实验面临严重的外部有效性问题：招募具有代表性的参与者样本往往困难或昂贵，导致实验结果难以推广到实际目标人群。同时，软件工程领域存在大量观察性数据（如代码仓库、问题追踪器、日志、调查和工业流程数据），但这些数据与实验数据的结合尚未得到充分利用。

Method: 1. 介绍transportability方法及其前提条件；2. 通过模拟实验演示其潜力；3. 概述该方法在软件工程中的多个适用场景（如学生替代开发者的有效性问题）；4. 制定路线图和实践指南以支持研究人员应用。

Result: 该方法能够将实验结果从实验样本"迁移"到更广泛、更具代表性的目标人群，从而增强外部有效性。模拟研究证明了该方法的可行性和潜在价值。

Conclusion: 在软件工程研究中采用transportability方法可以加强控制实验的外部有效性，帮助该领域产生更可靠、更具实践价值的研究成果。

Abstract: Controlled experiments are a core research method in software engineering (SE) for validating causal claims. However, recruiting a sample of participants that represents the intended target population is often difficult or expensive, which limits the external validity of experimental results. At the same time, SE researchers often have access to much larger amounts of observational than experimental data (e.g., from repositories, issue trackers, logs, surveys and industrial processes). Transportability methods combine these data from experimental and observational studies to "transport" results from the experimental sample to a broader, more representative sample of the target population. Although the ability to combine observational and experimental data in a principled way could substantially benefit empirical SE research, transportability methods have - to our knowledge - not been adopted in SE. In this vision, we aim to help make that adoption possible. To that end, we introduce transportability methods, their prerequisites, and demonstrate their potential through a simulation. We then outline several SE research scenarios in which these methods could apply, e.g., how to effectively use students as substitutes for developers. Finally, we outline a road map and practical guidelines to support SE researchers in applying them. Adopting transportability methods in SE research can strengthen the external validity of controlled experiments and help the field produce results that are both more reliable and more useful in practice.

</details>


### [224] [Empirical Evaluation of Taxonomic Trace Links: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08207)
*Waleed Abdeen,Michael Unterkalmsteiner,Peter Löwenadler,Parisa Yousefi,Krzysztof Wnuk*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究在爱立信公司对Taxonomic Trace Links (TTL)方法进行了工业实证评估。TTL通过领域特定分类法连接源工件和目标工件，旨在解决传统追溯性面临的粒度、结构异构性和责任不清等挑战。研究采用混合方法，涵盖463个业务用例、64个测试用例和277个ISO标准需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 追溯性是知识密集型任务中的关键质量属性，但实践中常被忽视，主要挑战包括：追溯链接的粒度问题、缺乏通用工件结构、以及责任不明确。TTL方法旨在通过领域特定分类法解决这些共性挑战。

Method: 在爱立信公司开展混合方法研究，对一款软件产品进行评估。通过两个追溯性用例收集定量和定性数据，建立了463个业务用例、64个测试用例和277个ISO标准需求之间的追溯链接，并组织了3场焦点小组讨论。

Result: 识别出两个需要追溯性的实用场景。总体而言，从业者认为TTL在一个场景中有用，另一个场景中用处有限。主要挑战包括：开发领域特定分类法、管理异构工件结构、以及分类器精度需改进。

Conclusion: TTL是一种有前景的方法，可用于实践并支持追溯性用例，但不是传统追溯链接的替代品，而是补充方案，能够实现更多追溯性用例并鼓励早期创建追溯链接。

Abstract: Context: Traceability is a key quality attribute of artifacts that are used in knowledge-intensive tasks and supports software engineers in producing higher-quality software. Despite its clear benefits, traceability is often neglected in practice due to challenges such as granularity of traces, lack of a common artifact structure, and unclear responsibility. The Taxonomic Trace Links (TTL) approach connects source and target artifacts through a domain-specific taxonomy, aiming to address these common traceability challenges. Objective: In this study, we empirically evaluate TTL in an industrial setting to identify its strengths and weaknesses for real-world adoption. Method: We conducted a mixed-methods study at Ericsson involving one of its software products. Quantitative and qualitative data were collected across two traceability use cases. We established trace links between 463 business use cases, 64 test cases, and 277 ISO-standard requirements. Additionally, we held three focus group sessions with practitioners. Results: We identified two practically relevant scenarios where traceability is required and evaluated TTL in each. Overall, practitioners found TTL to be a useful solution for one of the scenarios, while less useful for the other. However, developing a domain-specific taxonomy and managing heterogeneous artifact structures were noted as significant challenges. Moreover, the precision of the classifier that is used to create trace links needs to be improved to make the solution practical. Conclusion: TTL is a promising approach that can be adopted in practice and enables traceability use cases. However, TTL is not a replacement for traditional trace links, but rather complements them to enable more traceability use cases and encourage the early creation of trace links.

</details>


### [225] [Tokalator: A Context Engineering Toolkit for Artificial Intelligence Coding Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08290)
*Vahid Farajijobehdar,İlknur Köseoğlu Sarı,Nazım Kemal Üre,Engin Zeydan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Tokalator是一个开源的上下文工程工具包，为AI辅助编码环境提供实时令牌预算监控和优化功能。该工具包含VS Code扩展、11个斜杠命令、9个Web计算器、MCP服务器/CLI、Python API和PostgreSQL使用追踪器，支持17个LLM模型，已在Visual Studio Marketplace获得313次安装。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助编码环境虽然拥有128,000-1,000,000 tokens的上下文窗口，但现有工具对令牌消耗的监控和优化支持有限。当开发者打开多个文件时，模型注意力会被稀释，API成本随对话长度增加。研究发现指令文件注入和低相关性的打开标签页是主要的隐性预算消耗源。

Method: Tokalator采用多组件架构：VS Code扩展提供实时预算监控；Web计算器实现Cobb-Douglas质量建模、缓存盈亏平衡分析和O(T²)对话成本证明；社区目录收录agents、prompts和instruction文件；MCP服务器和CLI提供集成接口；Python econometrics API支持计量经济分析；PostgreSQL后端追踪使用情况。系统支持17个LLM，覆盖Anthropic、OpenAI、Google三个提供商，通过124个单元测试验证。

Result: v3.1.3版本在Visual Studio Marketplace获得313次安装，转化率达206.02%。对50名开发者进行的结构化调查显示，指令文件注入和低相关性打开标签页是典型AI辅助开发会话中的主要隐性预算消耗因素。

Conclusion: Tokalator通过提供全面的上下文工程工具，有效解决了AI辅助编码环境中的令牌消耗监控和优化问题。其多提供商支持、社区驱动的资源库和实证研究结果，为开发者优化API成本和模型注意力分配提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI)-assisted coding environments operate within finite context windows of 128,000-1,000,000 tokens (as of early 2026), yet existing tools offer limited support for monitoring and optimizing token consumption. As developers open multiple files, model attention becomes diluted and Application Programming Interface (API) costs increase in proportion to input and output as conversation length grows. Tokalator is an open-source context-engineering toolkit that includes a VS Code extension with real-time budget monitoring and 11 slash commands; nine web-based calculators for Cobb-Douglas quality modeling, caching break-even analysis, and $O(T^2)$ conversation cost proofs; a community catalog of agents, prompts, and instruction files; an MCP server and Command Line Interface (CLI); a Python econometrics API; and a PostgreSQL-backed usage tracker. The system supports 17 Large Language Models (LLMs) across three providers (Anthropic, OpenAI, Google) and is validated by 124 unit tests. An initial deployment on the Visual Studio Marketplace recorded 313 acquisitions with a 206.02\% conversion rate as of v3.1.3. A structured survey of 50 developers across three community sessions indicated that instruction-file injection and low-relevance open tabs are among the primary invisible budget consumers in typical AI-assisted development sessions.

</details>


### [226] [CIAO - Code In Architecture Out - Automated Software Architecture Documentation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08293)
*Marco De Luca,Tiziano Santilli,Domenico Amalfitano,Anna Rita Fasolino,Patrizio Pelliccione*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CIAO是一种基于LLM的自动化系统级架构文档生成流程，通过整合ISO/IEC/IEEE 42010、SEI Views & Beyond和C4模型标准，从GitHub仓库自动生成架构文档。22位开发者的评估表明该方法生成的文档具有实用价值、准确且成本低廉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件架构文档对系统理解至关重要，但现实中往往缺失或不完整。虽然现有LLM技术能从代码生成文档，但通常只处理局部构件，无法生成连贯的系统级架构描述。现有工具缺乏对整体系统架构的建模能力。

Method: CIAO流程采用结构化LLM工作流，以GitHub仓库为输入，基于ISO/IEC/IEEE 42010、SEI Views & Beyond和C4模型导出文档模板，生成可直接添加到目标仓库的系统级架构文档。

Result: 22名开发者评估显示：(1)生成的文档被认为有价值、易理解且与源代码基本准确；(2)局限性在于图表质量、高级上下文建模和部署视图；(3)运营成本低，完整文档生成仅需几分钟且成本低廉。

Conclusion: 结构化、基于标准的方法可有效指导LLM生成可用且成本效益高的系统级架构文档。该研究证明了将架构标准与LLM能力相结合用于自动化文档生成的可行性。

Abstract: Software architecture documentation is essential for system comprehension, yet it is often unavailable or incomplete. While recent LLM-based techniques can generate documentation from code, they typically address local artifacts rather than producing coherent, system-level architectural descriptions. This paper presents a structured process for automatically generating system-level architectural documentation directly from GitHub repositories using Large Language Models. The process, called CIAO (Code In Architecture Out), defines an LLM-based workflow that takes a repository as input and produces system-level architectural documentation following a template derived from ISO/IEC/IEEE 42010, SEI Views \& Beyond, and the C4 model. The resulting documentation can be directly added to the target repository. We evaluated the process through a study with 22 developers, each reviewing the documentation generated for a repository they had contributed to. The evaluation shows that developers generally perceive the produced documentation as valuable, comprehensible, and broadly accurate with respect to the source code, while also highlighting limitations in diagram quality, high-level context modeling, and deployment views. We also assessed the operational cost of the process, finding that generating a complete architectural document requires only a few minutes and is inexpensive to run. Overall, the results indicate that a structured, standards-oriented approach can effectively guide LLMs in producing system-level architectural documentation that is both usable and cost-effective.

</details>


### [227] [Security Concerns in Generative AI Coding Assistants: Insights from Online Discussions on GitHub Copilot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08352)
*Nicolás E. Díaz Ferreyra,Monika Swetha Gurupathi,Zadia Codabux,Nalin Arachchilage,Riccardo Scandariato*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析Stack Overflow、Reddit和Hacker News三个平台上的开发者讨论，识别出GitHub Copilot等GenAI编码助手存在的四类安全关切：数据泄露风险、代码许可问题、对抗性攻击（如提示注入）以及不安全代码建议，为改进GenAI安全功能提供参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦于GenAI代码生成的技术性能评估，而对开发者，特别是安全领域新兴关切的关注不足。该研究旨在填补这一空白，探索开发者对GenAI编码助手安全问题的真实担忧。

Method: 从Stack Overflow、Reddit、Hacker News三个平台检索涉及GitHub Copilot安全问题的帖子、评论和讨论帖；利用BERTopic进行主题聚类；结合主题分析法进行综合分析，识别安全关切类别。

Result: 识别出四大安全关切领域：潜在数据泄露、代码许可问题、对抗性攻击（如提示注入）以及不安全代码建议。这些发现揭示了开发者对GenAI在软件工程中局限性权衡的深刻反思。

Conclusion: 本研究深化了对开发者如何认知和参与GenAI编码助手的理解，同时突出了需要改进内置安全功能的关键领域，为GenAI工具的未来优化指明方向。

Abstract: Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) has become a central component of many development tools (e.g., GitHub Copilot) that support software practitioners across multiple programming tasks, including code completion, documentation, and bug detection. However, current research has identified significant limitations and open issues in GenAI, including reliability, non-determinism, bias, and copyright infringement. While prior work has primarily focused on assessing the technical performance of these technologies for code generation, less attention has been paid to emerging concerns of software developers, particularly in the security realm. OBJECTIVE: This work explores security concerns regarding the use of GenAI-based coding assistants by analyzing challenges voiced by developers and software enthusiasts in public online forums. METHOD: We retrieved posts, comments, and discussion threads addressing security issues in GitHub Copilot from three popular platforms, namely Stack Overflow, Reddit, and Hacker News. These discussions were clustered using BERTopic and then synthesized using thematic analysis to identify distinct categories of security concerns. RESULTS: Four major concern areas were identified, including potential data leakage, code licensing, adversarial attacks (e.g., prompt injection), and insecure code suggestions, underscoring critical reflections on the limitations and trade-offs of GenAI in software engineering. IMPLICATIONS: Our findings contribute to a broader understanding of how developers perceive and engage with GenAI-based coding assistants, while highlighting key areas for improving their built-in security features.

</details>


### [228] [Vulnerability Detection with Interprocedural Context in Multiple Languages: Assessing Effectiveness and Cost of Modern LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08417)
*Kevin Lira,Baldoino Fonseca,Davy Baía,Márcio Ribeiro,Wesley K. G. Assunção*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究首次系统性地评估了现代大语言模型（Claude Haiku 4.5、GPT-4.1 Mini、GPT-5 Mini、 Gemini 3 Flash）在检测跨函数依赖相关漏洞方面的有效性。研究对509个漏洞进行了实验，覆盖C、C++和Python三种语言，通过变化上下文级别（仅目标函数、目标函数+调用者、目标函数+被调用者）进行评估。结果显示Gemini 3 Flash在C语言漏洞检测上性价比最优（F1≥0.978，成本$0.50-$0.58/配置），Claude Haiku 4.5的识别和解释准确率达93.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM漏洞检测研究仅关注单函数内部漏洞，忽略了跨函数数据流和控制流产生的安全缺陷。调用者-被调用者上下文信息可能显著提升漏洞识别能力。

Method: 对509个ReposVul数据集中的漏洞进行实证研究，在C/C++/Python代码上测试4种现代LLM，系统性变化上下文级别：仅目标函数、目标函数+调用者、目标函数+被调用者，评估检测效果、推理成本和解释质量。

Result: Gemini 3 Flash对C语言漏洞检测性价比最优，F1≥0.978，估算成本$0.50-$0.58/配置；Claude Haiku 4.5正确识别并解释漏洞的比例达93.6%。

Conclusion: 研究结果对设计可跨代码库和多编程语言泛化的AI辅助安全分析工具具有直接指导意义。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have been a promising way for automated vulnerability detection. However, most prior studies have explored the use of LLMs to detect vulnerabilities only within single functions, disregarding those related to interprocedural dependencies. These studies overlook vulnerabilities that arise from data and control flows that span multiple functions. Thus, leveraging the context provided by callers and callees may help identify vulnerabilities. This study empirically investigates the effectiveness of detection, the inference cost, and the quality of explanations of four modern LLMs (Claude Haiku 4.5, GPT-4.1 Mini, GPT-5 Mini, and Gemini 3 Flash) in detecting vulnerabilities related to interprocedural dependencies. To do that, we conducted an empirical study on 509 vulnerabilities from the ReposVul dataset, systematically varying the level of interprocedural context (target function code-only, target function + callers, and target function + callees) and evaluating the four modern LLMs across C, C++, and Python. The results show that Gemini 3 Flash offers the best cost-effectiveness trade-off for C vulnerabilities, achieving F1 >= 0.978 at an estimated cost of $0.50-$0.58 per configuration, and Claude Haiku 4.5 correctly identified and explained the vulnerability in 93.6% of the evaluated cases. Overall, the findings have direct implications for the design of AI-assisted security analysis tools that can generalize across codebases in multiple programming languages.

</details>


### [229] [ReCodeAgent: A Multi-Agent Workflow for Language-agnostic Translation and Validation of Large-scale Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07341)
*Ali Reza Ibrahimzada,Brandon Paulsen,Daniel Kroening,Reyhaneh Jabbarvand*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ReCodeAgent是一个自主多智能体框架，用于实现跨编程语言的仓库级代码翻译与验证。通过多智能体协作，该方法首次在多种编程语言对（C-Rust、Go-Rust、Java-Python、Python-JavaScript）上实现了高翻译成功率，测试通过率提升60.8%，平均成本仅$15.3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前仓库级代码翻译技术局限于单一编程语言对，跨语言迁移需要大量工程适配工作。现有方法缺乏真正的编程语言无关性，无法在多种编程语言间实现自主、高效的代码翻译与验证。

Method: 提出ReCodeAgent框架，采用多智能体架构实现语言无关的仓库级代码翻译与验证。系统能够自主使用各编程语言特定的工具进行代码分析和验证，用户仅需提供源语言项目和目标语言即可完成全自动翻译。

Result: 在6种编程语言（C、Go、Java、JavaScript、Python、Rust）和4种语言对上的118个真实项目（平均1,975行代码，43个翻译单元）测试中，ReCodeAgent在翻译正确性上显著优于四种神经符号和智能体方法，测试通过率提升60.8%，平均成本$15.3。多智能体架构相比单智能体方案，测试通过率提升40.4%，轨迹长度缩短28%。

Conclusion: ReCodeAgent首次实现了跨多种编程语言的高成功率仓库级代码翻译。多智能体架构是实现高效翻译的关键设计选择，验证了智能体协作在编程语言无关代码翻译任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Most repository-level code translation and validation techniques have been evaluated on a single source-target programming language (PL) pair, owing to the complex engineering effort required to adapt new PL pairs. Programming agents can enable PL-agnosticism in repository-level code translation and validation: they can synthesize code across many PLs and autonomously use existing tools specific to each PL's analysis. However, state-of-the-art has yet to offer a fully autonomous agentic approach for repository-level code translation and validation of large-scale programs.
