This paper proposes ReCodeAgent, an autonomous multi-agent approach for language-agnostic repository-level code translation and validation. Users only need to provide the project in the source PL and specify the target PL for ReCodeAgent to automatically translate and validate the entire repository. ReCodeAgent is the first technique to achieve high translation success rates across many PLs.
  We compare the effectiveness of ReCodeAgent with four alternative neuro-symbolic and agentic approaches to translate 118 real-world projects, with 1,975 LoC and 43 translation units for each project, on average. The projects cover 6 PLs (C, Go, Java, JavaScript, Python, and Rust) and 4 PL pairs (C-Rust, Go-Rust, Java-Python, Python-JavaScript). Our results demonstrate that ReCodeAgent consistently outperforms prior techniques on translation correctness, improving test pass rate by 60.8% on ground-truth tests, with an average cost of $15.3. We also perform process-centric analysis of ReCodeAgent trajectories to confirm its procedural efficiency. Finally, we investigate how the design choices (a multi-agent vs. single-agent architecture) influence ReCodeAgent performance: on average, the test pass rate drops by 40.4%, and trajectories become 28% longer and persistently inefficient.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [230] [Blink: CPU-Free LLM Inference by Delegating the Serving Stack to GPU and SmartNIC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07609)
*Mohammad Siavashi,Mariano Scazzariello,Gerald Q. Maguire,Dejan Kostić,Marco Chiesa*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Blink是一种端到端的LLM服务架构，通过将请求处理卸载到SmartNIC并使用RDMA直接传输数据到GPU内存，同时用持久GPU内核替代主机驱动的调度，彻底将主机CPU从推理关键路径中移除，实现更高的吞吐量和能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM推理服务系统将主机CPU置于关键路径上，用于编排和逐token控制，导致：1) 性能对CPU干扰敏感；2) 限制了应用共置；3) 迫使运营商预留CPU冗余，造成容量浪费。

Method: Blink将职责重新分配到SmartNIC和GPU：1) 将请求处理卸载到SmartNIC；2) 通过RDMA将输入直接传送到GPU内存；3) 用持久GPU内核替代主机驱动的批处理、调度和KV-cache管理，完全无需CPU参与。

Result: 相比TensorRT-LLM、vLLM和SGLang：P99 TTFT降低最高8.47倍，P99 TPOT降低最高3.40倍，decode吞吐量提升最高2.1倍，每token能耗降低最高48.6%。在CPU干扰下，Blink保持稳定性能，而现有系统性能下降达两个数量级。

Conclusion: Blink成功将CPU从推理关键路径中移除，在隔离和干扰场景下均优于现有系统，显著提升性能、能效和稳定性。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) inference is rapidly becoming a core datacenter service, yet current serving stacks keep the host CPU on the critical path for orchestration and token-level control. This makes LLM performance sensitive to CPU interference, undermining application colocation and forcing operators to reserve CPU headroom, leaving substantial capacity unutilized.
  We introduce Blink, an end-to-end serving architecture that removes the host CPU from the steady-state inference path by redistributing responsibilities across a SmartNIC and a GPU. Blink offloads request handling to the SmartNIC, which delivers inputs directly into GPU memory via RDMA, and replaces host-driven scheduling with a persistent GPU kernel that performs batching, scheduling, and KV-cache management without CPU involvement.
  Evaluated against TensorRT-LLM, vLLM, and SGLang, Blink outperforms all baselines even in isolation, reducing pre-saturation P99 TTFT by up to 8.47$\times$ and P99 TPOT by up to 3.40$\times$, improving decode throughput by up to 2.1$\times$, and reducing energy per token by up to 48.6$\%$. Under CPU interference, Blink maintains stable performance, while existing systems degrade by up to two orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [231] [Reduced-Mass Orbital AI Inference via Integrated Solar, Compute, and Radiator Panels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07760)
*Stephen Gaalema,Samuel Indyk,Clinton Staley*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向太阳同步轨道（SSO）计算卫星的分布式计算架构，通过将太阳能电池、散热器和计算功能集成到多个面板单元中，实现>100 kW/吨的超高计算功率密度和~500 W/kg的太阳能比功率。该架构利用大面积蒸汽腔散热器将芯片结温控制在40°C左右，兼顾计算效率与可靠性。概念验证设计为16 MW计算能力、150吨质量的卫星，由16,000块面板组成（20m×2200m阵列），可部署在单艘星舰货舱中。每512面板子阵列支持128头注意力机制的500k上下文LLM推理，达到553 token/秒/会话、256并发会话的处理能力，整星可支持>7900个并发推理实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI推理需求的爆发式增长，传统地面数据中心面临能耗、延迟和地理限制等挑战。将计算能力部署到太空，利用太空微重力环境（强化散热效率）、近乎无限的太阳能资源和低延迟的全球覆盖优势，有望实现革命性的计算架构。

Method: 1. 架构设计：将太阳能电池、蒸汽腔散热器与计算芯片三维集成于面板单元，通过大规模面板阵列实现计算密度和散热能力的协同扩展；2. 热管理：利用太空真空环境的大面积蒸汽腔散热器，将芯片结温控制在~40°C，显著提升计算效率（每单位能耗更多有效计算）和器件可靠性；3. 部署方案：采用可折叠/卷绕的阵列设计，实现单艘SpaceX星舰整流罩内的运输（20m×2200m）；4. 性能评估：基于512面板子阵列和128头注意力机制的Transformer模型架构，评估LLM推理吞吐量。

Result: 1. 计算功率密度：>100 kW/吨（包含部署和轨道维持质量）；2. 太阳能比功率：~500 W/kg（传统方案<100 W/kg）；3. 单子阵列性能：553 token/sec/session，256并发会话；4. 整星性能：31个子阵列，>7900并发推理实例；5. 卫星规模：16 MW计算能力，150吨质量，16,000块面板。

Conclusion: 该架构通过功能集成和热管理创新，首次系统性地论证了百吨级太空计算基础设施的可行性。若核心技术（高比功率太阳能/散热集成面板、蒸汽腔热管）取得突破，此类太空计算卫星可成为未来AI推理基础设施的重要组成部分，尤其适用于对延迟敏感和高密度计算场景。

Abstract: We describe and analyze a distributed compute architecture for SSO computational satellites that can potentially provide >100 kW compute power per launched metric ton (including deployment and station keeping mass). The architecture co-locates and integrates the solar cells, radiator, and compute functions into multiple small panels arranged in a large array. The resultant large vapor chamber radiator area per panel should permit ICs to operate at junction temperatures near 40*C with benefits in compute efficiency and reliability. Using the structure of the radiator to support the solar cells may also yield a specific power of about 500 W/kg compared to less than 100 for existing conventional implementations. Assuming development of custom solutions for all components, a 16 MW computation, 150 ton satellite comprising a 20 m x 2200 m grid of 16,000 panels can fit in a single Starship hold. The concept is scalable to much larger satellites with higher mass payloads or using on-orbit assembly. We consider panel sizes from 1 to 4 m2 to allow trading vapor chamber heat transport with compute efficiency and inter-panel communication. Assuming a 1 kW/panel design, 512-panel subarrays of the satellite can run a representative inference-only LLM with 500,000 token context window and 128 attention blocks, at a rate of 553 tokens/sec/session, across 256 simultaneous in-flight sessions. A full satellite could support 31 such subarrays, for >7900 inferences at a time.

</details>


### [232] [Administrative Decentralization in Edge-Cloud Multi-Agent for Mobile Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07767)
*Senyao Li,Zhigang Zuo,Haozhao Wang,Junyu Chen,Zhanbo Jin,Ruixuan LI*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出AdecPilot，一种基于管理去中心化原则的边云多智能体框架，通过将高层战略设计与战术落地解耦，解决现有方案中云端集中管理导致的认知延迟问题。实验表明该方案任务成功率提升21.7%，云端token消耗降低37.5%，端到端延迟降低88.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边云协同框架将管理权集中于云端，设备仅被动执行，导致对实时UI动态的认知滞后，无法有效应对移动自动化场景的需求。

Method: AdecPilot采用三层核心设计：(1) UI无关的云端设计器生成抽象里程碑；(2) 双模态边缘团队具备自主战术规划和自校正能力，无需云端干预；(3) 层级隐式终止协议确保确定性停止，防止完成后幻觉。

Result: 相较于EcoAgent，任务成功率提升21.7%，云端token消耗降低37.5%；相较于CORE，端到端延迟降低88.9%。

Conclusion: 管理去中心化可有效重塑边缘智能体角色，实现云端战略规划与边缘战术执行的高效分离，兼顾性能与效率。

Abstract: Collaborative edge-cloud frameworks have emerged as the main- stream paradigm for mobile automation, mitigating the latency and privacy risks inherent to monolithic cloud agents. However, existing approaches centralize administration in the cloud while relegating the device to passive execution, inducing a cognitive lag regard- ing real-time UI dynamics. To tackle this, we introduce AdecPilot by applying the principle of administrative decentralization to the edge-cloud multi-agent framework, which redefines edge agency by decoupling high-level strategic designing from tactical grounding. AdecPilot integrates a UI-agnostic cloud designer generating ab- stract milestones with a bimodal edge team capable of autonomous tactical planning and self-correction without cloud intervention. Furthermore, AdecPilot employs a Hierarchical Implicit Termi- nation protocol to enforce deterministic stops and prevent post- completion hallucinations. Extensive experiments demonstrate pro- posed approach improves task success rate by 21.7% while reducing cloud token consumption by 37.5% against EcoAgent and decreas- ing end to end latency by 88.9% against CORE. The source code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Anonymous_code- B8AB.

</details>


### [233] [LegoDiffusion: Micro-Serving Text-to-Image Diffusion Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08123)
*Lingyun Yang,Suyi Li,Tianyu Feng,Xiaoxiao Jiang,Zhipeng Di,Weiyi Lu,Kan Liu,Yinghao Yu,Tao Lan,Guodong Yang,Lin Qu,Liping Zhang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LegoDiffusion提出将文本到图像生成的多模型扩散工作流分解为松耦合的模型执行节点，实现独立管理和调度，从而在集群规模上实现优化，性能提升最高达3倍请求率和8倍突发流量容忍度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有服务系统将扩散工作流作为不透明的整体进行资源调配，暴露内部数据流、阻止模型共享、强制粗粒度资源管理。

Method: 将工作流分解为可独立管理和调度的松耦合模型执行节点，实现显式管理单个模型推理。

Result: 支持细粒度优化：per-model scaling、model sharing、adaptive model parallelism。

Conclusion: 相比现有扩散工作流服务系统，吞吐量提升最高3倍，突发流量容忍度提升最高8倍。

Abstract: Text-to-image generation executes a diffusion workflow comprising multiple models centered on a base diffusion model. Existing serving systems treat each workflow as an opaque monolith, provisioning, placing, and scaling all constituent models together, which obscures internal dataflow, prevents model sharing, and enforces coarse-grained resource management. In this paper, we make a case for micro-serving diffusion workflows with LegoDiffusion, a system that decomposes a workflow into loosely coupled model-execution nodes that can be independently managed and scheduled. By explicitly managing individual model inference, LegoDiffusion unlocks cluster-scale optimizations, including per-model scaling, model sharing, and adaptive model parallelism. Collectively, LegoDiffusion outperforms existing diffusion workflow serving systems, sustaining up to 3x higher request rates and tolerating up to 8x higher burst traffic.

</details>


### [234] [Wattlytics: A Web Platform for Co-Optimizing Performance, Energy, and TCO in HPC Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08182)
*Ayesha Afzal,Georg Hager,Gerhard Wellein*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Wattlytics是一个基于浏览器的GPU决策支持系统，集成了基准驱动的性能扩展、DVFS感知功耗建模和多年度TCO分析，支持异构GPU架构（GH200、H100、L40S等）和科学工作负载配置，帮助用户进行集群设计和采购决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着GPU加速计算系统的计算需求和能耗不断增长，设计和运营决策变得愈发复杂。现有工具多为采购导向的计算器，缺乏集成化的多维度分析能力，导致用户在HPC集群设计和采购时难以做出明智决策。

Method: 开发了Wattlytics系统，具有以下核心功能：1）基准驱动的GPU性能扩展建模；2）DVFS感知分段功耗建模；3）多年度TCO分析；4）支持多种GPU架构和工作负载（GROMACS、AMBER）；5）多维度决策指标计算（TCO分解、单位TCO工作量、单位TCO功耗等）；6）设计空间探索、情景分析、敏感性分析（弹性系数、Sobol指数、蒙特卡洛方法）。

Result: 案例研究表明，在预算或能源约束条件下，优化部署的高能效GPU在整体成本效益上可优于高性能替代方案。系统能够量化分析不同部署策略（固定预算、固定GPU数量、固定性能、固定功率）下的权衡关系。

Conclusion: Wattlytics将HPC设计转变为信息充分、可解释的决策过程，帮助用户探索设计参数空间，识别成本和风险驱动因素，为集群设计提供科学依据。

Abstract: The escalating computational demands and energy footprint of GPU-accelerated computing systems complicate informed design and operational decisions. We present the first release of Wattlytics (https://wattlytics.netlify.app), an interactive, browser-based decision-support system. Unlike existing procurement-oriented calculators, Wattlytics uniquely integrates benchmark-driven GPU performance scaling, dynamic voltage and frequency scaling (DVFS)-aware piecewise power modeling, and multi-year total cost of ownership (TCO) analysis within a single interactive environment. Users can configure heterogeneous systems across contemporary GPU architectures (GH200, H100, L40S, L40, A40, A100, and L4), select representative scientific workloads (e.g., GROMACS, AMBER), and explore deployment scenarios under constraints such as energy prices, system lifetime, and frequency scaling. Wattlytics computes multidimensional decision metrics (TCO breakdown, work-per-TCO, power-per-TCO, and work-per-watt-per-TCO) and supports design-space exploration, what-if scenarios, sensitivity metrics (elasticity, Sobol indices, Monte Carlo) and collaborative features to guide realistic cluster design and procurement under uncertainty. We demonstrate selected scenarios comparing deployment strategies under different operational modes: ixed budget, fixed GPU count, fixed performance, and fixed power. Our case studies show that, under budget or energy constraints, optimally deployed energy-efficient GPUs can outperform higher-performance alternatives in overall cost-effectiveness. Wattlytics helps users explore the design parameter space and distinguish between cost- and risk-driving factors, turning HPC design into a well-informed and explainable decision-making process.

</details>


### [235] [Asynchronous Quantum Distributed Computing: Causality, Snapshots, and Global Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08298)
*Siddhartha Visveswara Jayanti,Anand Natarajan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了异步量子分布式系统中实现可分解为局部操作的原子量子全局操作的问题，基于经典的Chandy-Lamport快照算法设计了QGO算法，并证明Lamport的计算因果性论据在量子世界中仍然有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算领域长期关注如何有效实现需要跨多个量子组件协调的全局操作。传统的分布式算法（如Chandy-Lamport快照算法）已在经典计算中得到验证，但如何在量子系统中实现类似功能仍缺乏系统性的理论与算法支持。

Method: 基于Chandy和Lamport的经典快照算法，设计了量子分布式算法（QGO算法）来实现可分解的全局操作。该方法利用量子力学的基本原理，将全局操作分解为各组件上的局部操作序列。

Result: 分析表明，尽管由于量子纠缠的存在，因果性无法从标准量子态的标准描述中直接体现，但基于Lamport计算因果性的论证在量子世界中仍然有效。这为量子分布式计算提供了理论支撑。

Conclusion: 本研究提出了量子分布式计算的正式模型，以及全局操作行为的正式规范（该规范也可应用于经典场景，如随机算法）。研究结果为量子分布式系统的设计和分析奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We initiate the study of asynchronous quantum distributed systems, focusing on the case of implementing atomic quantum global operations that can be decomposed into a collection of local operations on the components of the system. A simple example of such an operation is a quantum snapshot in which the whole system is instantaneously measured. Based on the classical snapshot algorithm of Chandy and Lamport, we design a quantum distributed algorithm to implement such decomposable global operations, which we call the QGO Algorithm. The analysis of our algorithm shows that arguments based on Lamport's computational causality remain valid in the quantum world, even though, due to entanglement, causality is not manifest from the standard description of the system in terms of a (global) quantum state. Our other contributions include a formal model of quantum distributed computing, and a formal specification for the desired behavior of a global operation, which may be of interest even in classical settings (such as in the setting of randomized algorithms).

</details>


### [236] [City-Scale Visibility Graph Analysis via GPU-Accelerated HyperBall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08374)
*Alex Hodge,Melissa Barrientos Trinanes*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出一种结合三种技术（LEB128变长编码压缩存储、HyperBall概率距离估计、CUDA融合解码联合内核）将可视性图分析扩展至城市规模。相对于depthmapX实现239倍端到端加速，可处理48万格子（48亿边）的问题规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Visibility Graph Analysis (VGA)是理解空间配置如何塑造人类移动的关键方法，但其依赖全对BFS计算限制了在大规模研究区域的应用。需要一种可扩展至城市级别的VGA系统。

Method: 三种技术组合：(1) LEB128变长编码的增量压缩CSR存储，实现~4x压缩率并支持内存映射超出现有RAM的图；(2) HyperBall——基于HyperLogLog计数器传播的概率距离估计器，首次应用于可视性图，将BFS复杂度从O(N|E|)降至O(D|E|2^p)；(3) CUDA GPU加速内核，融合解码联合内核通过PCIe流式传输压缩图并在共享内存中完成LEB128解码。

Result: 使用depthmapX自身可视性算法确保边集一致性的条件下，在42,705格子规模下实现239倍端到端加速，并可扩展至236,000格子（48亿边）仅需137秒。HyperBall在p=10时，Visual Mean Depth与精确值达到Pearson r=0.999，中位相对误差仅1.7%。

Conclusion: 该系统可处理远超depthmapX实际限制的问题规模，通过LEB128压缩实现内存高效存储，HyperBall提供可扩展的概率近似，GPU加速实现硬件级并行计算。

Abstract: Visibility Graph Analysis (VGA) is a key space syntax method for understanding how spatial configuration shapes human movement, but its reliance on all-pairs BFS computation limits practical application to small study areas. We present a system that combines three techniques to scale VGA to city-scale problems: (i) delta-compressed CSR storage using LEB128 varint encoding, which achieves ~4x compression and enables memory-mapped graphs exceeding available RAM; (ii) HyperBall, a probabilistic distance estimator based on HyperLogLog counter propagation, applied here for the first time to visibility graphs, reducing BFS complexity from O(N|E|) to O(D|E|2^p); and (iii) GPU-accelerated CUDA kernels with a fused decode-union kernel that streams the compressed graph via PCIe and performs LEB128 decoding entirely in shared memory. HyperBall's iteration count equals the topological depth limit, so the radius-n analysis that practitioners already use as standard translates directly into proportional speedup -- unlike depthmapX, whose BFS time is invariant to depth setting due to the small diameter of visibility graphs. Using depthmapX's own visibility algorithm (sparkSieve2) to ensure identical edge sets, our tool achieves a 239x end-to-end speedup at 42,705 cells and scales to 236,000 cells (4.8 billion edges) in 137 seconds -- problem sizes far beyond depthmapX's practical limit. At p=10, Visual Mean Depth achieves Pearson r=0.999 with 1.7% median relative error across 20 matched configurations.

</details>


### [237] [Taming GPU Underutilization via Static Partitioning and Fine-grained CPU Offloading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08451)
*Gabin Schieffer,Ruimin Shi,Jie Ren,Ivy Peng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对多实例GPU（MIG）在高性能计算（HPC）工作负载中的有效性和局限性进行了系统级表征研究，通过科学计算（ NekRS、LAMMPS）、AI（Llama3）和量子计算（Qiskit）等真实应用评估，发现GPU共享虽能提升资源利用率，但存在干扰和资源粒度不匹配问题，并提出基于Nvlink-C2C的内存卸载方案加以解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU算力和内存容量的快速提升为多种工作负载带来机遇，但不同应用特性导致资源利用不平衡。Multi-Instance GPU（MIG）通过将GPU计算和内存资源划分为固定大小的切片并提供隔离，有望改善资源利用率，但其对HPC工作负载的有效性和局限性尚不明确。

Method: 采用真实应用（NekRS、LAMMPS、Llama3、Qiskit）对不同GPU共享方案进行全面的系统级表征分析，涵盖科学计算、AI和数据 analytics 应用。

Result: 1. GPU共享通过MIG可显著减少资源未充分利用，提升系统吞吐量和能效；2. 共享资源（如功耗调节）仍会导致干扰；3. 粗粒度资源调配与紧耦合计算和内存资源需求存在不匹配。

Conclusion: 提出利用缓存一致的Nvlink-C2C互连的内存卸载方案，以弥合粗粒度资源切片与应用需求之间的差距，减少资源未充分利用。该方案为解决MIG资源粒度不匹配问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Advances in GPU compute throughput and memory capacity brings significant opportunities to a wide range of workloads. However, efficiently utilizing these resources remains challenging, particularly because diverse application characteristics may result in imbalanced utilization. Multi-Instance GPU (MIG) is a promising approach to improve utilization by partitioning GPU compute and memory resources into fixed-size slices with isolation. Yet, its effectiveness and limitations in supporting HPC workloads remain an open question. We present a comprehensive system-level characterization of different GPU sharing options using real-world scientific, AI, and data analytics applications, including NekRS, LAMMPS, Llama3, and Qiskit. Our analysis reveals that while GPU sharing via MIG can significantly reduce resource underutilization, and enable system-level improvements in throughput and energy, interference still occurs through shared resources, such as power throttling. Our performance-resource scaling results indicate that coarse-grained provisioning for tightly coupled compute and memory resources often mismatches application needs. To address this mismatch, we propose a memory-offloading scheme that leverages the cache-coherent Nvlink-C2C interconnect to bridge the gap between coarse-grained resource slices and reduce resource underutilization.

</details>

### [238] [Assessing the Feasibility of a Video-Based Conversational Chatbot Survey for Measuring Perceived Cycling Safety: A Pilot Study in New York City](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07375)
*Feiyang Ren,Zhaoxi Zhang,Tamir Mendel,Takahiro Yabe*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Bicycle safety is important for bikeability and transportation efficiency. However, conventional surveys often fall short in capturing how people actually perceive cycling environments because they rely heavily on respondents' recall rather than in-the-moment experience. By leveraging large language models (LLMs), this study proposes a new method of combining video-based surveys with a conversational AI chatbot to collect human perceptions of cycling safety and the reasons behind these perceptions. The paper developed the AI chatbot using a modular LLM architecture, integrating prompt engineering, state management, and rule-based control to support the structure of human-AI interaction. This paper evaluates the feasibility of the proposed video-based conversational chatbot using complete responses from sixteen participants to the pilot survey across nine street segments in New York City. The method feasibility was assessed using a seven-point scale rating for user experience (i.e., ease of use, supportiveness, efficiency) and a five-point scale for chatbot usability (i.e., personality, roboticness, friendliness), yielding positive results with mean scores of 5.00 out of 7 (standard deviation = 1.6) and 3.47 out of 5 (standard deviation = 0.43), respectively. The data feasibility was assessed using multiple techniques: (1) Natural language processing (NLP), such as KeyBERT, for overall safety and feature analysis to extract built-environment attributes; (2) K-means clustering for semantic analysis to identify reasons and suggestions; and (3) regression to estimate the effects of built-environment and demographic variables on perceived safety outcomes. The results show the potential of AI chatbots as a novel approach to collecting data on human perception, behavior, and future visions for transport planning.

</details>


### [239] [Google, AI Literacy, and the Learning Sciences: Multiple Modes of Research, Industry, and Practice Partnerships](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07601)
*Victor R. Lee,Michael Madaio,Ben Garside,Aimee Welch,Kristen Pilner Blair,Ibrahim Oluwajoba Adisa,Alon Harris,Kevin Holst,Liat Ben Rafael,Ronit Levavi Morad,Ben Travis,Belle Moller,Andrew Shields,Zak Brown,Lois Hinx,Marisol Diaz,Evan Patton,Selim Tezel,Robert Parks,Hal Abelson,Adam Blasioli,Jeremy Roschelle*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文是一个研讨会摘要，探讨学术界与产业界（如Google）在AI素养教育方面的合作模式，通过多个合作项目案例，分析研究与实践的交集点、影响因素及未来机会。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前在普通人群中大规模推广AI素养教育面临复杂挑战，需要多方利益相关者协作。作者认为产业界（尤其是科技公司）是AI领域的重要参与者，学术界与产业界有潜力成为实现共同目标的合作伙伴。然而，关于如何建立有效合作的研究和实践探索仍然不足。

Method: 采用研讨会形式，结合主题演讲、评论和小组讨论，呈现Google参与的多个AI素养合作项目作为案例分析的基础。

Result: 期望识别出三个核心问题的答案：(1) 研究、实践与产业合作在AI素养教育生命周期中的交集点；(2) 塑造合作方向性重点的因素和历史背景；(3) 对各方均有益的新合作模式的机会所在。

Conclusion: 通过建立学术界与产业界的合作伙伴关系，可以更有效地推进AI素养教育。研讨会旨在总结现有合作经验，为未来建立新的、互惠互利的合作配置提供参考框架。

Abstract: Enabling AI literacy in the general population at scale is a complex challenge requiring multiple stakeholders and institutions collaborating together. Industry and technology companies are important actors with respect to AI, and as a field, we have the opportunity to consider how researchers and companies might be partners toward shared goals. In this symposium, we focus on a collection of partnership projects that all involve Google and all address AI literacy as a comparative set of examples. Through a combination of presentations, commentary, and moderated group discussion, the session, we will identify (1) at what points in the life cycle do research, practice, and industry partnerships clearly intersect; (2) what factors and histories shape the directional focus of the partnerships; and (3) where there may be future opportunities for new configurations of partnership that are jointly beneficial to all parties.

</details>


### [240] [The Weaponization of Computer Vision: Tracing Military-Surveillance Ties through Conference Sponsorship](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07803)
*Noa Garcia,Amelia Katirai*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究计算机视觉研究在军事和监控领域的武器化程度，通过分析顶级CV会议赞助商发现44%的赞助商与军事或监控应用有直接关联，揭示了该领域在伦理透明度方面的严重缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机视觉领域虽起源于军事资助并被系统性武器化，却自我定位为中立的纯技术领域，缺乏对双重用途的伦理讨论。作者认为需要实证研究来揭示该领域与军事监控应用的关联程度。

Method: 收集CV会议赞助商数据，分析赞助商的经营活动，辅以两个案例研究来探讨赞助作为揭示技术武器化手段的可行性与局限性。

Result: 44%的会议赞助商与军事或监控应用存在直接关联，证明该领域与国防安全部门的深度融合远超公众认知。

Conclusion: 会议赞助不仅是公司投资该领域的强有力证据，更赋予其塑造领域发展轨迹的特权地位。研究揭示了通过赞助商分析揭示技术武器化的机会与局限，为AI伦理治理提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Computer vision, a core domain of artificial intelligence (AI), is the field that enables the computational analysis, understanding, and generation of visual data. Despite being historically rooted in military funding and increasingly deployed in warfare, the field tends to position itself as a neutral, purely technical endeavor, failing to engage in discussions about its dual-use applications. Yet it has been reported that computer vision systems are being systematically weaponized to assist in technologies that inflict harm, such as surveillance or warfare. Expanding on these concerns, we study the extent to which computer vision research is being used in the military and surveillance domains. We do so by collecting a dataset of tech companies with financial ties to the field's central research exchange platform: conferences. Conference sponsorship, we argue, not only serves as strong evidence of a company's investment in the field but also provides a privileged position for shaping its trajectory. By investigating sponsors' activities, we reveal that 44% of them have a direct connection with military or surveillance applications. We extend our analysis through two case studies in which we discuss the opportunities and limitations of sponsorship as a means for uncovering technological weaponization.

</details>


### [241] [We Need Strong Preconditions For Using Simulations In Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07838)
*Steven Luo,Saanvi Arora,Carlos Guirado*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文讨论了LLM智能体模拟在政策制定中的应用潜力和两大核心挑战（双重用途风险与输出验证困难），并提出三项预防措施作为负责任开发的边界框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM模拟作为政策工具潜力巨大，但面临双重用途风险（可能被滥用于模拟弱势群体）和模拟输出验证困难两大关键挑战，亟需建立明确的伦理边界和使用规范。

Method: 提出三项预防性原则作为社会规模LLM智能体模拟的前提条件：（1）不将边缘化群体的模拟视为中立的技術输出；（2）不未经参与模拟人群；（3）不匿名模拟并要求问责。同时呼吁制定模拟开发与部署报告制度。

Result: 建立了三项预防措施框架，明确了模拟开发者与决策者的责任边界；提出需建立模拟开发与部署报告机制以增强透明度。

Conclusion: 三项预防措施结合报告制度，有助于在促进LLM智能体模拟负责任开发与部署的同时，建立政策制定者对这一工具的信任，最终实现服务于公共利益的目标。

Abstract: Simulations, and more recently LLM agent simulations, have been adopted as useful tools for policymakers to explore interventions, rehearse potential scenarios, and forecast outcomes. While LLM simulations have enormous potential, two critical challenges remain understudied: the dual-use potential of accurate models of individual or population-level human behavior and the difficulty of validating simulation outputs. In light of these limitations, we must define boundaries for both simulation developers and decision-makers to ensure responsible development and ethical use. We propose and discuss three preconditions for societal-scale LLM agent simulations: 1) do not treat simulations of marginalized populations as neutral technical outputs, 2) do not simulate populations without their participation, and 3) do not simulate without accountability. We believe that these guardrails, combined with our call for simulation development and deployment reports, will help build trust among policymakers while promoting responsible development and use of societal-scale LLM agent simulations for the public benefit.

</details>


### [242] [Co-design for Trustworthy AI: An Interpretable and Explainable Tool for Type 2 Diabetes Prediction Using Genomic Polygenic Risk Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08217)
*Ralf Beuthan,Megan Coffee,Heejin Kim,Na Yeon Kim,Pedro Kringen,Elisabeth Hildt,Haekyung Lee,Seunggeun Lee,Emilie Wiinblad Mathez,Sira Maliphol,Vadim Pak,Yuna Park,Stephan Sonnenberg,Jesmin Jahan Tithi,Magnus Westerlund,Roberto V. Zicari*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究针对2型糖尿病(T2DM)的多基因风险评分(PRS)缺乏可解释性的问题，提出了可解释的XPRS工具，利用SHAP值分解PRS为基因水平和SNP水平的贡献分数，并通过Z-inspection和HUDERIA框架评估其可信度，为临床应用中AI系统的设计提供伦理、法律和技术指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多基因风险评分(PRS)在量化复杂疾病遗传易感性方面取得显著进展，但现有的PRS工具普遍缺乏可解释性，无法帮助临床医生和患者理解具体遗传因素如何驱动疾病风险，这限制了PRS在临床实践中的广泛应用和信任度。

Method: 1) 开发XPRS工具，采用Shapley Additive Explanations (SHAP)方法将PRS分解为基因级别和SNP级别的贡献分数；2) 采用协同设计(co-design)方法，使用Z-inspection方法论和Council of Europe的HUDERIA框架，从法律、医学、伦理和技术鲁棒性角度评估工具可信度；3) 多学科专家团队(伦理学、法学、人权、计算机科学和医学)参与评审。

Result: 建立了AI系统设计的多学科伦理、法律和技术框架，识别出一套全面的经验教训，为PRS可解释性工具在不同临床场景中的开发提供了实践指导。

Conclusion: XPRS为T2DM的PRS提供了细粒度的遗传因素解释能力，协同设计方法和多学科评估框架可有效识别AI系统的潜在风险和信任问题，为未来临床AI系统的可信部署提供了方法论参考。

Abstract: The polygenic risk scores (PRS) have emerged as an important methodology for quantifying genetic predisposition to complex traits and clinical disease. Significant progress has been made in applying PRS to conditions such as obesity, cancer, and type 2 diabetes (T2DM). Studies have demonstrated that PRS can effectively identify individuals at high risk, thereby enabling early screening, personalized treatment, and targeted interventions for diseases with a genetic predisposition. One current limitation of PRS, however, is the lack of interpretability tools. To address this problem for T2DM, researchers at the Graduate School of Data Science at the Seoul National University introduced eXplainable PRS (XPRS). This visualization tool decomposes PRSs into gene-level and single-nucleotide polymorphism (SNP) contribution scores via Shapley Additive Explanations (SHAP), providing granular insights into the specific genetic factors driving an individual's risk profile. We used a co-design approach to assess XPRS trustworthiness by considering legal, medical, ethical, and technical robustness during early design and potential clinical use. For that, we used Z-inspection, an ethically aligned Trustworthy AI co-design methodology, and piloted the Council of Europe's Human Rights, Democracy, and the Rule of Law Impact Assessment for AI Systems (HUDERIA) (Council of Europe (CAI) 2025). The findings of this use-case comprise a comprehensive set of ethical, legal, and technical lessons learned. These insights, identified by a multidisciplinary team of experts (ethics, legal, human rights, computer science, and medical), serve as a framework for designers to navigate future challenges with this and other AI systems. The findings also provide a useful reference for researchers developing explainability frameworks for PRS in diverse clinical contexts.

</details>


### [243] [The Statistical Profitability of Social Media Sports Betting Influencers: Evidence from the Nigerian Market](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08251)
*Kayode Makinde,Oluwatimileyin Onasanya,Frances Adelakun*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究追踪了尼日利亚三位知名体育博彩影响者在社交媒体上推广的5,467个投注单，总金额约480万美元。研究发现这些影响者自身亏损25.24%，而跟随者若等额投注则会亏损38.27%，四种投注策略模拟均显示持续亏损，揭示了社交媒体博彩宣传与实际收益之间的巨大鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在解决"幸存者偏差"问题——博彩影响者通常只展示赢钱的投注，扭曲公众认知。通过系统追踪真实投注记录，评估跟随社交媒体博彩影响者进行投注是否为明智的财务策略，并揭示尼日利亚快速扩张的博彩市场中潜在的消费者保护问题。

Method: 研究团队追踪了三位X（原Twitter）和Telegram平台上知名博彩分析师推广的5,467个赛前投注单，通过官方Stake.com记录验证结果，形成最终数据集。使用原始表现分析、按赔率大小评估风险，并应用四种常见投注策略（平注、逆向、平 方根、固定回报）模拟跟随者的真实收益。

Result: 研究揭示了表面财富展示与实际财务结果之间的尖锐对比。影响者自身在推广的投注中亏损25.24%；若投资者对每个推荐等额投注，则亏损达38.27%。在所有测试的投注策略下，跟随这些影响者均导致显著财务亏损。

Conclusion: 研究结果对尼日利亚扩张中的博彩市场提出了严重的消费者保护担忧。研究揭示了社交媒体博彩影响者营销的潜在危害，强调需要加强监管以保护易受影响的受众群体。

Abstract: This study examines whether following popular Nigerian sports betting influencers on social media is a financially sound strategy. To avoid the survivorship bias that occurs when influencers only share their winning bets, we tracked 5,467 pre-match betting slips from three prominent tipsters on X (formerly Twitter) and Telegram. We verified the outcomes against official Stake.com records, resulting in a final dataset covering approximately $4.8 million in tracked bets. We analyzed raw performance, assessed risk based on odds sizes, and applied four common staking strategies (Flat, Inverse, Square Root, and Fixed Return) to simulate realistic follower outcomes. The results show a sharp contrast between the wealth these influencers display online and the actual financial results. The influencers themselves collectively lost 25.24% on their promoted bets, while a follower who staked the same amount on every tip would lose 38.27% on their investment. Across all tested strategies, following these influencers consistently led to significant financial losses. These findings raise serious consumer protection concerns in Nigeria's expanding gambling market.

</details>


### [244] [Navigating Turbulence: The Challenge of Inclusive Innovation in the U.S.-China AI Race](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08353)
*Jyh-An Lee,Jingwen Liu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究了中美地缘政治竞争对人工智能发展包容性的影响，通过比较分析数据隐私、知识产权和出口管制三个法律维度，揭示两国如何通过各自的法律框架塑造AI竞争格局，并指出这种技术 rivalry 已导致全球范围内的排他性规则制定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中美在AI领域的地缘政治竞争日益激烈，但现有研究缺乏对两国法律基础设施如何系统影响AI创新的比较分析。本文旨在填补这一空白，探讨法律框架差异对AI包容性发展的深层影响。

Method: 采用比较法律分析方法，对美国和中国在AI领域的法律基础设施进行三个维度的比较研究：数据隐私、知识产权保护、出口管制措施。通过对三国法律环境的系统性对比，分析各自的优势与劣势。

Result: 研究指出：中国在数据获取和知识产权保护方面具有一定法律优势；美国则通过严格的半导体芯片、AI模型出口管制以及对外投资限制维持技术优势。两国法律框架的差异共同塑造了AI竞争格局，并催生了全球性的排他性规则体系。

Conclusion: 中美两国在数据隐私、知识产权和出口管制方面的法律差异深刻影响着AI竞争态势。中国在数据获取方面更具优势，而美国在关键技术出口管控方面占据上风。这种竞争态势已导致全球AI治理呈现碎片化趋势，对包容性创新构成挑战。

Abstract: This chapter examines the impact of the geopolitical rivalry between the United States and China on the prospects for inclusive innovation in artificial intelligence (AI) development. We explore three critical aspects of the American and Chinese legal infrastructure that significantly impact AI innovation: data privacy, intellectual property (IP rights), and export restrictions. Through this comparative analysis, we argue that, while China's legal environment may offer certain advantage in terms of access to training data and IP protection, the United States maintains superior resources by enforcing strict export controls on semiconductor chips, AI models, as well as outbound investments in these areas. This nuanced examination helps illuminate how each country's legal framework could influence the ultimate trajectory of AI race and how the technological rivalry has led to exclusionary rulemaking on a global scale.
